n22 src/Surface.zig:899-920
  899  fn queueIo(
  900      self: *Surface,
  901      msg: termio.Message,
  902      mutex: termio.Termio.MutexState,
  903  ) void {
  904      // In readonly mode, we don't allow any writes through to the pty.
  905      if (self.readonly) {
  906          switch (msg) {
  907              .write_small,
  908              .write_stable,
  909              .write_alloc,
  910              => {
  911                  msg.deinit();
  912                  return;
  913              },
  914  
  915              else => {},
  916          }
  917      }
  918  
  919      self.io.queueMessage(msg, mutex);
  920  }

  182  pub const DerivedConfig = struct {
  183      arena: ArenaAllocator,
  184  
  185      palette: terminalpkg.color.Palette,
  186      image_storage_limit: usize,
  187      cursor_style: terminalpkg.CursorStyle,
  188      cursor_blink: ?bool,
  189      cursor_color: ?configpkg.Config.TerminalColor,
  190      foreground: configpkg.Config.Color,
  191      background: configpkg.Config.Color,
  192      osc_color_report_format: configpkg.Config.OSCColorReportFormat,
  193      clipboard_write: configpkg.ClipboardAccess,
  194      clipboard_write_limit: usize,
  195      enquiry_response: []const u8,
  196      xt_checksum_report: bool,
  197      xt_checksum_extension: terminalpkg.xt_checksum.Flags,
  198      conditional_state: configpkg.ConditionalState,
  199  
  200      pub fn init(
  201          alloc_gpa: Allocator,
  202          config: *const configpkg.Config,
  203      ) !DerivedConfig {
  204          var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(alloc_gpa);
  205          errdefer arena.deinit();
  206          const alloc = arena.allocator();
  207  
  208          const palette: terminalpkg.color.Palette = palette: {
  209              if (config.@"palette-generate") generate: {
  210                  if (config.palette.mask.findFirstSet() == null) {
  211                      // If the user didn't set any values manually, then
  212                      // we're using the default palette and we don't need
  213                      // to apply the generation code to it.
  214                      break :generate;
  215                  }
  216  
  217                  break :palette terminalpkg.color.generate256Color(config.palette.value, config.palette.mask, config.background.toTerminalRGB(), config.foreground.toTerminalRGB(), config.@"palette-harmonious");
  218              }
  219  
  220              break :palette config.palette.value;
  221          };
  222  
  223          return .{
  224              .palette = palette,
  225              .image_storage_limit = config.@"image-storage-limit",
  226              .cursor_style = config.@"cursor-style",
  227              .cursor_blink = config.@"cursor-style-blink",
  228              .cursor_color = config.@"cursor-color",
  229              .foreground = config.foreground,
  230              .background = config.background,
  231              .osc_color_report_format = config.@"osc-color-report-format",
  232              .clipboard_write = config.@"clipboard-write",
  233              .clipboard_write_limit = config.@"clipboard-write-limit-bytes".value,
  234              .enquiry_response = try alloc.dupe(u8, config.@"enquiry-response"),
  235              .xt_checksum_report = config.@"vt-xt-checksum-report",
  236              .xt_checksum_extension = xtChecksumFlags(config.@"vt-xt-checksum-extension"),
  237              .conditional_state = config._conditional_state,
  238  
  239              // This has to be last so that we copy AFTER the arena allocations
  240              // above happen (Zig assigns in order).
  241              .arena = arena,
  242          };
  243      }
  244  
  245      pub fn deinit(self: *DerivedConfig) void {
  246          self.arena.deinit();
  247      }
  248  };

  254  pub fn init(self: *Termio, alloc: Allocator, opts: termio.Options) !void {
  255      // The default terminal modes based on our config.
  256      const default_modes: terminalpkg.ModePacked = modes: {
  257          var modes: terminalpkg.ModePacked = .{};
  258  
  259          // Setup our initial grapheme cluster support if enabled. We use a
  260          // switch to ensure we get a compiler error if more cases are added.
  261          switch (opts.full_config.@"grapheme-width-method") {
  262              .unicode => modes.grapheme_cluster = true,
  263              .legacy => {},
  264          }
  265  
  266          // Set default cursor blink settings
  267          modes.cursor_blinking = opts.config.cursor_blink orelse true;
  268  
  269          break :modes modes;
  270      };
  271  
  272      // Create our terminal
  273      var term = try terminalpkg.Terminal.init(global.io(), alloc, opts: {
  274          const grid_size = opts.size.grid();
  275          break :opts .{
  276              .cols = grid_size.columns,
  277              .rows = grid_size.rows,
  278              .max_scrollback_bytes = opts.full_config.@"scrollback-limit-bytes".optional(),
  279              .max_scrollback_lines = opts.full_config.@"scrollback-limit-lines".optional(),
  280              .default_modes = default_modes,
  281              .default_cursor_style = opts.config.cursor_style,
  282              .default_cursor_blink = opts.config.cursor_blink,
  283              .default_xt_checksum = opts.config.xt_checksum_extension,
  284              .colors = .{
  285                  .background = .init(opts.config.background.toTerminalRGB()),
  286                  .foreground = .init(opts.config.foreground.toTerminalRGB()),
  287                  .cursor = cursor: {
  288                      const color = opts.config.cursor_color orelse break :cursor .unset;
  289                      const rgb = color.toTerminalRGB() orelse break :cursor .unset;
  290                      break :cursor .init(rgb);
  291                  },
  292                  .palette = .default,
  293              },
  294              .kitty_image_storage_limit = opts.config.image_storage_limit,
  295              .kitty_image_loading_limits = .allWithTempDir(global.tmpDirPath()),
  296          };
  297      });
  298      errdefer term.deinit(alloc);
  299  
  300      // The default palette may be an allocator-owned copy, so it is set
  301      // once the terminal owns its memory and can release it on deinit.
  302      try term.colors.palette.changeDefault(alloc, opts.config.palette);
  303  
  304      // Setup our terminal size in pixels for certain requests.
  305      term.width_px = term.cols * opts.size.cell.width;
  306      term.height_px = term.rows * opts.size.cell.height;
  307  
  308      // Setup our backend.
  309      var backend = opts.backend;
  310      backend.initTerminal(&term);
  311  
  312      // Create our stream handler. This points to memory in self so it
  313      // isn't safe to use until self.* is set.
  314      const handler: StreamHandler = .{
  315          .alloc = alloc,
  316          .termio_mailbox = &self.mailbox,
  317          .surface_mailbox = opts.surface_mailbox,
  318          .renderer_state = opts.renderer_state,
  319          .renderer_wakeup = opts.renderer_wakeup,
  320          .renderer_mailbox = opts.renderer_mailbox,
  321          .size = &self.size,
  322          .terminal = &self.terminal,
  323          .osc_color_report_format = opts.config.osc_color_report_format,
  324          .clipboard_write = opts.config.clipboard_write,
  325          .clipboard_write_limit = opts.config.clipboard_write_limit,
  326          .enquiry_response = opts.config.enquiry_response,
  327          .xt_checksum_report = opts.config.xt_checksum_report,
  328      };
  329  
  330      const thread_enter_state = try ThreadEnterState.create(
  331          alloc,
  332          opts.full_config,
  333      );
  334  
  335      self.* = .{
  336          .alloc = alloc,
  337          .terminal = term,
  338          .config = opts.config,
  339          .renderer_state = opts.renderer_state,
  340          .renderer_wakeup = opts.renderer_wakeup,
  341          .renderer_mailbox = opts.renderer_mailbox,
  342          .surface_mailbox = opts.surface_mailbox,
  343          .size = opts.size,
  344          .backend = backend,
  345          .mailbox = opts.mailbox,
  346          .terminal_stream = .init(.{
  347              .allocator = alloc,
  348              .handler = handler,
  349          }),
  350          .thread_enter_state = thread_enter_state,
  351      };
  352  }
  353  
  354  pub fn deinit(self: *Termio) void {
  355      self.backend.deinit();
  356      self.terminal.deinit(self.alloc);
  357      self.config.deinit();
  358      self.mailbox.deinit(self.alloc);
  359  
  360      // Clear any StreamHandler state
  361      self.terminal_stream.deinit();
  362  
  363      // Clear any initial state if we have it
  364      if (self.thread_enter_state) |v| v.destroy();
  365  }

n22 src/termio/Termio.zig:427-463
  427  pub fn threadExit(self: *Termio, data: *ThreadData) void {
  428      self.backend.threadExit(data);
  429  }
  430  
  431  /// Send a message to the mailbox. Depending on the mailbox type in use
  432  /// this may process now or it may just enqueue and process later.
  433  ///
  434  /// This will also notify the mailbox thread to process the message. If
  435  /// you're sending a lot of messages, it may be more efficient to use
  436  /// the mailbox directly and then call notify separately.
  437  pub fn queueMessage(
  438      self: *Termio,
  439      msg: termio.Message,
  440      mutex: MutexState,
  441  ) void {
  442      self.mailbox.send(msg, switch (mutex) {
  443          .locked => self.renderer_state.mutex,
  444          .unlocked => null,
  445      });
  446      self.mailbox.notify();
  447  }
  448  
  449  /// Queue a write directly to the pty.
  450  ///
  451  /// If you're using termio.Thread, this must ONLY be called from the
  452  /// mailbox thread. If you're not on the thread, use queueMessage with
  453  /// mailbox messages instead.
  454  ///
  455  /// If you're not using termio.Thread, this is not threadsafe.
  456  pub inline fn queueWrite(
  457      self: *Termio,
  458      td: *ThreadData,
  459      data: []const u8,
  460      linefeed: bool,
  461  ) !void {
  462      try self.backend.queueWrite(self.alloc, td, data, linefeed);
  463  }
n22 src/termio/Termio.zig:466-481
  466  pub fn changeConfig(self: *Termio, td: *ThreadData, config: *DerivedConfig) !void {
  467      // The remainder of this function is modifying terminal state or
  468      // the read thread data, all of which requires holding the renderer
  469      // state lock.
  470      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  471      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  472  
  473      // Deinit our old config. We do this in the lock because the
  474      // stream handler may be referencing the old config (i.e. enquiry resp)
  475      self.config.deinit();
  476      self.config = config.*;
  477  
  478      // Update our stream handler. The stream handler uses the same
  479      // renderer mutex so this is safe to do despite being executed
  480      // from another thread.
  481      self.terminal_stream.handler.changeConfig(&self.config);
n22 src/termio/Termio.zig:524-564
  524  pub fn resize(
  525      self: *Termio,
  526      td: *ThreadData,
  527      size: renderer.Size,
  528  ) !void {
  529      self.size = size;
  530      const grid_size = size.grid();
  531  
  532      // Enter the critical area that we want to keep small
  533      {
  534          self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  535          defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  536  
  537          // Resizing the pty can immediately wake the shell with SIGWINCH.
  538          // Hold the same lock used by processOutput before notifying it, so
  539          // its redraw cannot be parsed against the old terminal dimensions.
  540          try self.backend.resize(grid_size, size.terminal());
  541  
  542          // Update the size of our terminal state
  543          try self.terminal.resize(
  544              self.alloc,
  545              .{
  546                  .cols = grid_size.columns,
  547                  .rows = grid_size.rows,
  548                  .cell_size_px = .{
  549                      .width = self.size.cell.width,
  550                      .height = self.size.cell.height,
  551                  },
  552              },
  553          );
  554  
  555          // If we have size reporting enabled we need to send a report.
  556          if (self.terminal.modes.get(.in_band_size_reports)) {
  557              try self.sizeReportLocked(td, .mode_2048);
  558          }
  559      }
  560  
  561      // Mail the renderer so that it can update the GPU and re-render
  562      _ = self.renderer_mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .resize = size }, .{ .forever = {} });
  563      self.renderer_wakeup.notify() catch {};
  564  }
n22 src/termio/Termio.zig:705-762
  705  pub fn processOutput(self: *Termio, buf: []const u8) void {
  706      // We are modifying terminal state from here on out and we need
  707      // the lock to grab our read data.
  708      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  709      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  710      self.processOutputLocked(buf);
  711  }
  712  
  713  /// Process output from readdata but the lock is already held.
  714  fn processOutputLocked(self: *Termio, buf: []const u8) void {
  715      // Schedule a render. We can call this first because we have the lock.
  716      self.terminal_stream.handler.queueRender() catch unreachable;
  717  
  718      // Whenever a character is typed, we ensure the cursor is in the
  719      // non-blink state so it is rendered if visible. If we're under
  720      // HEAVY read load, we don't want to send a ton of these so we
  721      // use a timer under the covers
  722      const now = std.Io.Timestamp.now(global.io(), .awake);
  723      cursor_reset: {
  724          if (self.last_cursor_reset) |last| {
  725              if (last.durationTo(now).toMilliseconds() <= 500) {
  726                  break :cursor_reset;
  727              }
  728          }
  729  
  730          self.last_cursor_reset = now;
  731          _ = self.renderer_mailbox.push(global.io(), .{
  732              .reset_cursor_blink = {},
  733          }, .{ .instant = {} });
  734      }
  735  
  736      // If we have an inspector, we enter SLOW MODE because we need to
  737      // process a byte at a time alternating between the inspector handler
  738      // and the termio handler. This is very slow compared to our optimizations
  739      // below but at least users only pay for it if they're using the inspector.
  740      if (self.renderer_state.inspector) |insp| {
  741          for (buf, 0..) |byte, i| {
  742              insp.recordPtyRead(
  743                  self.alloc,
  744                  &self.terminal,
  745                  buf[i .. i + 1],
  746              ) catch |err| {
  747                  log.err("error recording pty read in inspector err={}", .{err});
  748              };
  749  
  750              self.terminal_stream.next(byte);
  751          }
  752      } else {
  753          self.terminal_stream.nextSlice(buf);
  754      }
  755  
  756      // If our stream handling caused messages to be sent to the mailbox
  757      // thread, then we need to wake it up so that it processes them.
  758      if (self.terminal_stream.handler.termio_messaged) {
  759          self.terminal_stream.handler.termio_messaged = false;
  760          self.mailbox.notify();
  761      }
  762  }

   47          }
   48      }

   51          switch (self.*) {
   52              .exec => |*exec| exec.threadExit(td),
   53          }
   54      }

n22 src/termio/Exec.zig:86-104
   86  pub fn threadEnter(
   87      self: *Exec,
   88      alloc: Allocator,
   89      io: *termio.Termio,
   90      td: *termio.Termio.ThreadData,
   91  ) !void {
   92      // Start our subprocess
   93      const pty_fds = self.subprocess.start(alloc) catch |err| {
   94          // If we specifically got this error then we are in the forked
   95          // process and our child failed to execute. If we DIDN'T
   96          // get this specific error then we're in the parent and
   97          // we need to bubble it up.
   98          if (err != error.ExecFailedInChild) return err;
   99  
  100          // We're in the child. Nothing more we can do but abnormal exit.
  101          // The Command will output some additional information.
  102          std.process.exit(1);
  103      };
  104      errdefer self.subprocess.stop();
n22 src/termio/Exec.zig:123-156
  123      // Create our pipe that we'll use to kill our read thread.
  124      // pipe[0] is the read end, pipe[1] is the write end.
  125      const pipe = try internal_os.pipe();
  126      errdefer _ = posix.system.close(pipe[0]);
  127      errdefer _ = posix.system.close(pipe[1]);
  128  
  129      // Setup our stream so that we can write.
  130      var stream = xev.Stream.initFd(pty_fds.write);
  131      errdefer stream.deinit();
  132  
  133      // Start our timer to read termios state changes. This is used
  134      // to detect things such as when password input is being done
  135      // so we can render the terminal in a different way.
  136      var termios_timer = try xev.Timer.init();
  137      errdefer termios_timer.deinit();
  138  
  139      // Start our read thread
  140      const read_thread = try std.Thread.spawn(
  141          .{},
  142          if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) ReadThread.threadMainWindows else ReadThread.threadMainPosix,
  143          .{ pty_fds.read, io, pipe[0] },
  144      );
  145      read_thread.setName(global.io(), "io-reader") catch {};
  146  
  147      // Setup our threadata backend state to be our own
  148      td.backend = .{ .exec = .{
  149          .start = process_start,
  150          .write_stream = stream,
  151          .process = process,
  152          .read_thread = read_thread,
  153          .read_thread_pipe = pipe[1],
  154          .read_thread_fd = pty_fds.read,
  155          .termios_timer = termios_timer,
  156      } };
n22 src/termio/Exec.zig:196-230
  196  pub fn threadExit(self: *Exec, td: *termio.Termio.ThreadData) void {
  197      assert(td.backend == .exec);
  198      const exec = &td.backend.exec;
  199  
  200      if (exec.exited) self.subprocess.externalExit();
  201      self.subprocess.stop();
  202  
  203      // Quit our read thread after exiting the subprocess so that
  204      // we don't get stuck waiting for data to stop flowing if it is
  205      // a particularly noisy process.
  206      switch (posix.errno(posix.system.write(exec.read_thread_pipe, "x", 1))) {
  207          .SUCCESS => {},
  208  
  209          // EPIPE means that our read thread is closed already, which is
  210          // completely fine since that is what we were trying to achieve.
  211          .PIPE => {},
  212  
  213          else => |e| log.warn(
  214              "error writing to read thread quit pipe err=E{s}",
  215              .{@tagName(e)},
  216          ),
  217      }
  218  
  219      if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) {
  220          // Interrupt the blocking read so the thread can see the quit message
  221          if (windows.exp.kernel32.CancelIoEx(exec.read_thread_fd, null) == windows.FALSE) {
  222              switch (windows.GetLastError()) {
  223                  .NOT_FOUND => {},
  224                  else => |err| log.warn("error interrupting read thread err={}", .{err}),
  225              }
  226          }
  227      }
  228  
  229      exec.read_thread.join();
  230  }

n22 src/termio/Thread.zig:253-279
  253      // Get the mailbox. This must be an SPSC mailbox for threading.
  254      const mailbox = switch (io.mailbox) {
  255          .spsc => |*v| v,
  256          // else => return error.TermioUnsupportedMailbox,
  257      };
  258  
  259      // This is the data sent to xev callbacks. We want a pointer to both
  260      // ourselves and the thread data so we can thread that through (pun intended).
  261      var cb: CallbackData = .{ .self = self, .io = io };
  262  
  263      // Run our thread start/end callbacks. This allows the implementation
  264      // to hook into the event loop as needed. The thread data is created
  265      // on the stack here so that it has a stable pointer throughout the
  266      // lifetime of the thread.
  267      try io.threadEnter(self, &cb.data);
  268      defer cb.data.deinit();
  269      defer io.threadExit(&cb.data);
  270  
  271      // Start the async handlers.
  272      mailbox.wakeup.wait(&self.loop, &self.wakeup_c, CallbackData, &cb, wakeupCallback);
  273      self.stop.wait(&self.loop, &self.stop_c, CallbackData, &cb, stopCallback);
  274  
  275      // Run
  276      log.debug("starting IO thread", .{});
  277      defer log.debug("starting IO thread shutdown", .{});
  278      try self.loop.run(.until_done);
  279  }
n22 src/termio/Thread.zig:289-375
  289  fn drainMailbox(
  290      self: *Thread,
  291      cb: *CallbackData,
  292  ) !void {
  293      // We assert when starting the thread that this is the state
  294      const mailbox = cb.io.mailbox.spsc.queue;
  295      const io = cb.io;
  296      const data = &cb.data;
  297  
  298      // If we're draining, we just drain the mailbox and return.
  299      if (self.flags.drain) {
  300          while (mailbox.pop(global.io())) |msg| msg.deinit();
  301          return;
  302      }
  303  
  304      // This holds the mailbox lock for the duration of the drain. The
  305      // expectation is that all our message handlers will be non-blocking
  306      // ENOUGH to not mess up throughput on producers.
  307      var redraw: bool = false;
  308      while (mailbox.pop(global.io())) |message| {
  309          // If we have a message we always redraw
  310          redraw = true;
  311  
  312          log.debug("mailbox message={s}", .{@tagName(message)});
  313          switch (message) {
  314              .color_scheme_report => |v| try io.colorSchemeReport(data, v.force),
  315              .visibility_report => |v| try io.visibilityReport(
  316                  data,
  317                  v.visible,
  318                  v.force,
  319              ),
  320              .crash => @panic("crash request, crashing intentionally"),
  321              .change_config => |config| {
  322                  defer config.alloc.destroy(config.ptr);
  323                  try io.changeConfig(data, config.ptr);
  324              },
  325              .inspector => |v| self.flags.has_inspector = v,
  326              .resize => |v| self.handleResize(cb, v),
  327              .size_report => |v| try io.sizeReport(data, v),
  328              .clear_screen => |v| try io.clearScreen(data, v.history),
  329              .scroll_viewport => |v| io.scrollViewport(v),
  330              .selection_scroll => |v| {
  331                  if (v) {
  332                      self.startScrollTimer(cb);
  333                  } else {
  334                      self.stopScrollTimer();
  335                  }
  336              },
  337              .jump_to_prompt => |v| try io.jumpToPrompt(v),
  338              .kitty_clipboard_grant_read => |v| {
  339                  defer v.alloc.free(v.pw);
  340                  try io.kittyClipboardGrant(v.pw, .read);
  341              },
  342              .kitty_clipboard_grant_write => |v| {
  343                  defer v.alloc.free(v.pw);
  344                  try io.kittyClipboardGrant(v.pw, .write);
  345              },
  346              .start_synchronized_output => self.startSynchronizedOutput(cb),
  347              .linefeed_mode => |v| self.flags.linefeed_mode = v,
  348              .focused => |v| try io.focusGained(data, v),
  349              .write_small => |v| try io.queueWrite(
  350                  data,
  351                  v.data[0..v.len],
  352                  self.flags.linefeed_mode,
  353              ),
  354              .write_stable => |v| try io.queueWrite(
  355                  data,
  356                  v,
  357                  self.flags.linefeed_mode,
  358              ),
  359              .write_alloc => |v| {
  360                  defer v.alloc.free(v.data);
  361                  try io.queueWrite(
  362                      data,
  363                      v.data,
  364                      self.flags.linefeed_mode,
  365                  );
  366              },
  367          }
  368      }
  369  
  370      // Trigger a redraw after we've drained so we don't waste cyces
  371      // messaging a redraw.
  372      if (redraw) {
  373          try io.renderer_wakeup.notify();
  374      }
  375  }

n23 src/termio/Exec.zig:86-104
   86  pub fn threadEnter(
   87      self: *Exec,
   88      alloc: Allocator,
   89      io: *termio.Termio,
   90      td: *termio.Termio.ThreadData,
   91  ) !void {
   92      // Start our subprocess
   93      const pty_fds = self.subprocess.start(alloc) catch |err| {
   94          // If we specifically got this error then we are in the forked
   95          // process and our child failed to execute. If we DIDN'T
   96          // get this specific error then we're in the parent and
   97          // we need to bubble it up.
   98          if (err != error.ExecFailedInChild) return err;
   99  
  100          // We're in the child. Nothing more we can do but abnormal exit.
  101          // The Command will output some additional information.
  102          std.process.exit(1);
  103      };
  104      errdefer self.subprocess.stop();
n23 src/termio/Exec.zig:123-156
  123      // Create our pipe that we'll use to kill our read thread.
  124      // pipe[0] is the read end, pipe[1] is the write end.
  125      const pipe = try internal_os.pipe();
  126      errdefer _ = posix.system.close(pipe[0]);
  127      errdefer _ = posix.system.close(pipe[1]);
  128  
  129      // Setup our stream so that we can write.
  130      var stream = xev.Stream.initFd(pty_fds.write);
  131      errdefer stream.deinit();
  132  
  133      // Start our timer to read termios state changes. This is used
  134      // to detect things such as when password input is being done
  135      // so we can render the terminal in a different way.
  136      var termios_timer = try xev.Timer.init();
  137      errdefer termios_timer.deinit();
  138  
  139      // Start our read thread
  140      const read_thread = try std.Thread.spawn(
  141          .{},
  142          if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) ReadThread.threadMainWindows else ReadThread.threadMainPosix,
  143          .{ pty_fds.read, io, pipe[0] },
  144      );
  145      read_thread.setName(global.io(), "io-reader") catch {};
  146  
  147      // Setup our threadata backend state to be our own
  148      td.backend = .{ .exec = .{
  149          .start = process_start,
  150          .write_stream = stream,
  151          .process = process,
  152          .read_thread = read_thread,
  153          .read_thread_pipe = pipe[1],
  154          .read_thread_fd = pty_fds.read,
  155          .termios_timer = termios_timer,
  156      } };

  587  const Subprocess = struct {
  588      const c = @import("posix_c");
  589  
  590      arena: std.heap.ArenaAllocator,
  591      cwd: ?[:0]const u8,
  592      env: ?EnvMap,
  593      args: []const [:0]const u8,
  594      grid_size: renderer.GridSize,
  595      screen_size: renderer.ScreenSize,
  596      pty: ?Pty = null,
  597      process: ?Process = null,
  598  
  599      rt_pre_exec_info: Command.RtPreExecInfo,
  600      rt_post_fork_info: Command.RtPostForkInfo,
  601  
  602      /// Union that represents the running process type.
  603      const Process = union(enum) {
  604          /// Standard POSIX fork/exec
  605          fork_exec: Command,
  606  
  607          /// Flatpak DBus command
  608          flatpak: FlatpakHostCommand,

n23 src/termio/Exec.zig:887-1095
  887      pub fn deinit(self: *Subprocess) void {
  888          self.stop();
  889          if (self.pty) |*pty| pty.deinit();
  890          if (self.env) |*env| env.deinit();
  891          self.arena.deinit();
  892          self.* = undefined;
  893      }
  894  
  895      /// Start the subprocess. If the subprocess is already started this
  896      /// will crash.
  897      pub fn start(self: *Subprocess, alloc: Allocator) !struct {
  898          read: Pty.Fd,
  899          write: Pty.Fd,
  900      } {
  901          assert(self.pty == null and self.process == null);
  902  
  903          // This function is funny because on POSIX systems it can
  904          // fail in the forked process. This is flipped to true if
  905          // we're in an error state in the forked process (child
  906          // process).
  907          var in_child: bool = false;
  908  
  909          // Create our pty
  910          var pty = try Pty.open(.{
  911              .ws_row = @intCast(self.grid_size.rows),
  912              .ws_col = @intCast(self.grid_size.columns),
  913              .ws_xpixel = @intCast(self.screen_size.width),
  914              .ws_ypixel = @intCast(self.screen_size.height),
  915          });
  916          self.pty = pty;
  917          errdefer if (!in_child) {
  918              if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag != .windows) {
  919                  _ = posix.system.close(pty.slave);
  920              }
  921  
  922              pty.deinit();
  923              self.pty = null;
  924          };
  925  
  926          // Cleanup we only run in our parent when we successfully start
  927          // the process.
  928          defer if (!in_child and self.process != null) {
  929              if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag != .windows) {
  930                  // Once our subcommand is started we can close the slave
  931                  // side. This prevents the slave fd from being leaked to
  932                  // future children.
  933                  _ = posix.system.close(pty.slave);
  934              }
  935  
  936              // Successful start we can clear out some memory.
  937              if (self.env) |*env| {
  938                  env.deinit();
  939                  self.env = null;
  940              }
  941          };
  942  
  943          log.debug("starting command command={f}", .{ArgsFormatter{ .args = self.args }});
  944  
  945          // If we can't access the cwd, then don't set any cwd and inherit.
  946          // This is important because our cwd can be set by the shell (OSC 7)
  947          // and we don't want to break new windows.
  948          const cwd: ?[:0]const u8 = if (self.cwd) |proposed| cwd: {
  949              if ((comptime build_config.flatpak) and internal_os.isFlatpak()) {
  950                  // Flatpak sandboxing prevents access to certain reserved paths
  951                  // regardless of configured permissions. Perform a test spawn
  952                  // to get around this problem
  953                  //
  954                  // https://docs.flatpak.org/en/latest/sandbox-permissions.html#reserved-paths
  955                  log.info("flatpak detected, will use host command to verify cwd access", .{});
  956                  const dev_null = try std.Io.Dir.cwd().openFile(
  957                      global.io(),
  958                      "/dev/null",
  959                      .{ .mode = .read_write },
  960                  );
  961                  defer dev_null.close(global.io());
  962                  var cmd: internal_os.FlatpakHostCommand = .{
  963                      .argv = &[_][]const u8{
  964                          "/bin/sh",
  965                          "-c",
  966                          ":",
  967                      },
  968                      .cwd = proposed,
  969                      .stdin = dev_null.handle,
  970                      .stdout = dev_null.handle,
  971                      .stderr = dev_null.handle,
  972                  };
  973                  _ = cmd.spawn(alloc) catch |err| {
  974                      log.warn("cannot spawn command at cwd, ignoring: {}", .{err});
  975                      break :cwd null;
  976                  };
  977                  _ = try cmd.wait();
  978  
  979                  break :cwd proposed;
  980              }
  981  
  982              if (std.Io.Dir.cwd().access(global.io(), proposed, .{})) {
  983                  break :cwd proposed;
  984              } else |err| {
  985                  log.warn("cannot access cwd, ignoring: {}", .{err});
  986                  break :cwd null;
  987              }
  988          } else null;
  989  
  990          // In flatpak, we use the HostCommand to execute our shell.
  991          if (internal_os.isFlatpak()) flatpak: {
  992              if (comptime !build_config.flatpak) {
  993                  log.warn("flatpak detected, but flatpak support not built-in", .{});
  994                  break :flatpak;
  995              }
  996  
  997              // Flatpak command must have a stable pointer.
  998              self.process = .{ .flatpak = .{
  999                  .argv = self.args,
 1000                  .cwd = cwd,
 1001                  .env = if (self.env) |*env| env else null,
 1002                  .stdin = pty.slave,
 1003                  .stdout = pty.slave,
 1004                  .stderr = pty.slave,
 1005              } };
 1006              var cmd = &self.process.?.flatpak;
 1007              const pid = try cmd.spawn(alloc);
 1008              errdefer killCommandFlatpak(cmd);
 1009  
 1010              log.info("started subcommand on host via flatpak API path={s} pid={}", .{
 1011                  self.args[0],
 1012                  pid,
 1013              });
 1014  
 1015              return .{
 1016                  .read = pty.master,
 1017                  .write = pty.master,
 1018              };
 1019          }
 1020  
 1021          // Build our subcommand
 1022          var cmd: Command = .{
 1023              .path = self.args[0],
 1024              .args = self.args,
 1025              .env = if (self.env) |*env| env else null,
 1026              .cwd = cwd,
 1027              .stdin = if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) null else .{
 1028                  .handle = pty.slave,
 1029                  .flags = .{ .nonblocking = false },
 1030              },
 1031              .stdout = if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) null else .{
 1032                  .handle = pty.slave,
 1033                  .flags = .{ .nonblocking = false },
 1034              },
 1035              .stderr = if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) null else .{
 1036                  .handle = pty.slave,
 1037                  .flags = .{ .nonblocking = false },
 1038              },
 1039              .pseudo_console = if (builtin.target.os.tag == .windows) pty.pseudo_console else {},
 1040              .os_pre_exec = switch (comptime builtin.target.os.tag) {
 1041                  .windows => null,
 1042                  else => f: {
 1043                      const f = struct {
 1044                          fn callback(cmd: *Command) ?u8 {
 1045                              const sp = cmd.getData(Subprocess) orelse unreachable;
 1046                              sp.childPreExec() catch |err| log.err(
 1047                                  "error initializing child: {}",
 1048                                  .{err},
 1049                              );
 1050                              return null;
 1051                          }
 1052                      };
 1053                      break :f f.callback;
 1054                  },
 1055              },
 1056              .rt_pre_exec = if (comptime @hasDecl(apprt.runtime, "pre_exec")) apprt.runtime.pre_exec.preExec else null,
 1057              .rt_pre_exec_info = self.rt_pre_exec_info,
 1058              .rt_post_fork = if (comptime @hasDecl(apprt.runtime, "post_fork")) apprt.runtime.post_fork.postFork else null,
 1059              .rt_post_fork_info = self.rt_post_fork_info,
 1060              .data = self,
 1061          };
 1062  
 1063          cmd.start(alloc) catch |err| {
 1064              // We have to do this because start on Windows can't
 1065              // ever return ExecFailedInChild
 1066              const StartError = error{ExecFailedInChild} || @TypeOf(err);
 1067              switch (@as(StartError, err)) {
 1068                  // If we fail in our child we need to flag it so our
 1069                  // errdefers don't run.
 1070                  error.ExecFailedInChild => {
 1071                      in_child = true;
 1072                      return err;
 1073                  },
 1074  
 1075                  else => return err,
 1076              }
 1077          };
 1078          errdefer killCommand(&cmd) catch |err| {
 1079              log.warn("error killing command during cleanup err={}", .{err});
 1080          };
 1081          log.info("started subcommand path={s} pid={?}", .{ self.args[0], cmd.pid });
 1082  
 1083          self.process = .{ .fork_exec = cmd };
 1084          return switch (builtin.target.os.tag) {
 1085              .windows => .{
 1086                  .read = pty.out_pipe,
 1087                  .write = pty.in_pipe,
 1088              },
 1089  
 1090              else => .{
 1091                  .read = pty.master,
 1092                  .write = pty.master,
 1093              },
 1094          };
 1095      }

n23 src/pty.zig:134-187
  134      pub fn open(size: winsize) OpenError!Pty {
  135          // Need to copy so that it becomes non-const.
  136          var sizeCopy = size;
  137  
  138          var master_fd: Fd = undefined;
  139          var slave_fd: Fd = undefined;
  140          if (c.openpty(
  141              &master_fd,
  142              &slave_fd,
  143              null,
  144              null,
  145              @ptrCast(&sizeCopy),
  146          ) < 0)
  147              return error.OpenptyFailed;
  148          errdefer {
  149              _ = posix.system.close(master_fd);
  150              _ = posix.system.close(slave_fd);
  151          }
  152  
  153          // Set CLOEXEC on the master fd, only the slave fd should be inherited
  154          // by the child process (shell/command).
  155          cloexec: {
  156              const flags = posix.system.fcntl(master_fd, posix.F.GETFD);
  157              if (flags == -1) {
  158                  log.warn("error getting flags for master fd err=E{s}", .{@tagName(posix.errno(-1))});
  159                  break :cloexec;
  160              }
  161  
  162              switch (posix.errno(posix.system.fcntl(
  163                  master_fd,
  164                  posix.F.SETFD,
  165                  flags | posix.FD_CLOEXEC,
  166              ))) {
  167                  .SUCCESS => {},
  168                  else => |e| log.warn("error setting CLOEXEC on master fd err=E{}", .{e}),
  169              }
  170          }
  171  
  172          // Enable UTF-8 mode. I think this is on by default on Linux but it
  173          // is NOT on by default on macOS so we ensure that it is always set.
  174          var attrs: c.termios = undefined;
  175          if (c.tcgetattr(master_fd, &attrs) != 0)
  176              return error.OpenptyFailed;
  177          attrs.c_iflag |= c.IUTF8;
  178          if (c.tcsetattr(master_fd, c.TCSANOW, &attrs) != 0)
  179              return error.OpenptyFailed;
  180  
  181          return .{
  182              .master = master_fd,
  183              .slave = slave_fd,
  184              .tty_name_buf = undefined,
  185              .tty_name = null,
  186          };
  187      }
n23 src/pty.zig:344-455
  344      pub fn open(size: winsize) OpenError!Pty {
  345          var pty: Pty = undefined;
  346  
  347          var pipe_path_buf: [128]u8 = undefined;
  348          var pipe_path_buf_w: [128]u16 = undefined;
  349          const pipe_path = std.mem.printSentinel(
  350              &pipe_path_buf,
  351              "\\\\.\\pipe\\LOCAL\\ghostty-pty-{d}-{d}",
  352              .{
  353                  windows.GetCurrentProcessId(),
  354                  pipe_name_counter.fetchAdd(1, .monotonic),
  355              },
  356              0,
  357          ) catch unreachable;
  358  
  359          const pipe_path_w_len = std.unicode.utf8ToUtf16Le(
  360              &pipe_path_buf_w,
  361              pipe_path,
  362          ) catch unreachable;
  363          pipe_path_buf_w[pipe_path_w_len] = 0;
  364          const pipe_path_w = pipe_path_buf_w[0..pipe_path_w_len :0];
  365  
  366          const security_attributes = windows.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES{
  367              .nLength = @sizeOf(windows.SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES),
  368              .bInheritHandle = windows.FALSE,
  369              .lpSecurityDescriptor = null,
  370          };
  371  
  372          pty.in_pipe = windows.exp.kernel32.CreateNamedPipeW(
  373              pipe_path_w.ptr,
  374              windows.PIPE_ACCESS_OUTBOUND |
  375                  windows.FILE_FLAG_FIRST_PIPE_INSTANCE |
  376                  windows.FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED,
  377              windows.PIPE_TYPE_BYTE,
  378              1,
  379              4096,
  380              4096,
  381              0,
  382              &security_attributes,
  383          );
  384          if (pty.in_pipe == windows.INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
  385              return windows.unexpectedError(windows.GetLastError());
  386          }
  387          errdefer _ = windows.exp.kernel32.CloseHandle(pty.in_pipe);
  388  
  389          var security_attributes_read = security_attributes;
  390          pty.in_pipe_pty = windows.exp.kernel32.CreateFileW(
  391              pipe_path_w.ptr,
  392              windows.GENERIC_READ,
  393              0,
  394              &security_attributes_read,
  395              windows.OPEN_EXISTING,
  396              windows.FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL,
  397              null,
  398          );
  399          if (pty.in_pipe_pty == windows.INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
  400              return windows.unexpectedError(windows.GetLastError());
  401          }
  402          errdefer _ = windows.exp.kernel32.CloseHandle(pty.in_pipe_pty);
  403  
  404          // The in_pipe needs to be created as a named pipe, since anonymous
  405          // pipes created with CreatePipe do not support overlapped operations,
  406          // and the IOCP backend of libxev only uses overlapped operations on files.
  407          //
  408          // It would be ideal to use CreatePipe here, so that our pipe isn't
  409          // visible to any other processes.
  410  
  411          // if (windows.exp.kernel32.CreatePipe(&pty.in_pipe_pty, &pty.in_pipe, null, 0) == 0) {
  412          //     return windows.unexpectedError(windows.kernel32.GetLastError());
  413          // }
  414          // errdefer {
  415          //     _ = windows.CloseHandle(pty.in_pipe_pty);
  416          //     _ = windows.CloseHandle(pty.in_pipe);
  417          // }
  418  
  419          if (windows.exp.kernel32.CreatePipe(&pty.out_pipe, &pty.out_pipe_pty, null, 0) == windows.FALSE) {
  420              return windows.unexpectedError(windows.GetLastError());
  421          }
  422          errdefer {
  423              _ = windows.exp.kernel32.CloseHandle(pty.out_pipe);
  424              _ = windows.exp.kernel32.CloseHandle(pty.out_pipe_pty);
  425          }
  426  
  427          const SetHandleInformation = struct {
  428              fn f(hObject: windows.HANDLE) !void {
  429                  if (windows.exp.kernel32.SetHandleInformation(
  430                      hObject,
  431                      windows.HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT,
  432                      0,
  433                  ) == windows.FALSE) {
  434                      return windows.unexpectedError(windows.GetLastError());
  435                  }
  436              }
  437          };
  438  
  439          try SetHandleInformation.f(pty.in_pipe);
  440          try SetHandleInformation.f(pty.in_pipe_pty);
  441          try SetHandleInformation.f(pty.out_pipe);
  442          try SetHandleInformation.f(pty.out_pipe_pty);
  443  
  444          const result = windows.exp.kernel32.CreatePseudoConsole(
  445              .{ .X = @intCast(size.ws_col), .Y = @intCast(size.ws_row) },
  446              pty.in_pipe_pty,
  447              pty.out_pipe_pty,
  448              0,
  449              &pty.pseudo_console,
  450          );
  451          if (result != windows.S_OK) return error.Unexpected;
  452  
  453          pty.size = size;
  454          return pty;
  455      }
n23 src/Command.zig:163-262
  163  pub fn start(self: *Command, alloc: Allocator) !void {
  164      // Use an arena allocator for the temporary allocations we need in this func.
  165      // IMPORTANT: do all allocation prior to the fork(). I believe it is undefined
  166      // behavior if you malloc between fork and exec. The source of the Zig
  167      // stdlib seems to verify this as well as Go.
  168      var arena_allocator = std.heap.ArenaAllocator.init(alloc);
  169      defer arena_allocator.deinit();
  170      const arena = arena_allocator.allocator();
  171  
  172      switch (builtin.target.os.tag) {
  173          .windows => try self.startWindows(arena),
  174          else => try self.startPosix(arena),
  175      }
  176  }
  177  
  178  fn startPosix(self: *Command, arena: Allocator) !void {
  179      // Null-terminate all our arguments
  180      const argsZ = try arena.allocSentinel(?[*:0]const u8, self.args.len, null);
  181      for (self.args, 0..) |arg, i| argsZ[i] = arg.ptr;
  182  
  183      // Determine our env vars
  184      const envp = if (self.env) |env_map|
  185          (try createNullDelimitedEnvMap(arena, env_map)).ptr
  186      else if (builtin.link_libc)
  187          std.c.environ
  188      else
  189          @compileError("missing env vars");
  190  
  191      // Fork.
  192      const pid = try fork();
  193  
  194      if (pid != 0) {
  195          // Parent, return immediately.
  196          self.pid = @intCast(pid);
  197          if (self.rt_post_fork) |f| try f(self);
  198          return;
  199      }
  200  
  201      // We are the child.
  202  
  203      // Setup our file descriptors for std streams.
  204      if (self.stdin) |f| setupFd(f.handle, posix.STDIN_FILENO) catch
  205          return error.ExecFailedInChild;
  206      if (self.stdout) |f| setupFd(f.handle, posix.STDOUT_FILENO) catch
  207          return error.ExecFailedInChild;
  208      if (self.stderr) |f| setupFd(f.handle, posix.STDERR_FILENO) catch
  209          return error.ExecFailedInChild;
  210  
  211      // Setup our working directory.
  212      //
  213      // NOTE: this can fail if we don't have permission to go to this directory
  214      // or if due to race conditions it doesn't exist or any various other
  215      // reasons. We don't want to crash the entire process if this fails so we
  216      // ignore it. We don't log because that'll show up in the output.
  217      if (self.cwd) |cwd| _ = posix.system.chdir(cwd);
  218  
  219      // Restore any rlimits that were set by Ghostty. This might fail but
  220      // any failures are ignored (its best effort).
  221      global.rlimits().restore();
  222  
  223      // If there are pre exec callbacks, call them now.
  224      if (self.os_pre_exec) |f| if (f(self)) |exitcode| posix.system.exit(exitcode);
  225      if (self.rt_pre_exec) |f| if (f(self)) |exitcode| posix.system.exit(exitcode);
  226  
  227      const err: posix.E = execve: {
  228          // This functionality has been taken from Zig stdlib, a simplified
  229          // version of the exec bits with PATH search so that we can just
  230          // offload to execve below.
  231          const file_slice = std.mem.sliceTo(self.path, 0);
  232          if (std.mem.findScalar(u8, file_slice, '/') != null) {
  233              break :execve posix.errno(posix.system.execve(self.path, argsZ, envp));
  234          }
  235  
  236          var path_expanded_buf: [std.Io.Dir.max_path_bytes]u8 = undefined;
  237          const PATH = global.environ().getPosix("PATH") orelse "/usr/local/bin:/bin/:/usr/bin";
  238          var it = std.mem.tokenizeScalar(u8, PATH, ':');
  239          var err: posix.system.E = .NOENT;
  240          var seen_eacces = false;
  241  
  242          while (it.next()) |search_path| {
  243              const path_len = search_path.len + file_slice.len + 1;
  244              if (path_expanded_buf.len < path_len + 1) break :execve .NAMETOOLONG;
  245              @memcpy(path_expanded_buf[0..search_path.len], search_path);
  246              path_expanded_buf[search_path.len] = '/';
  247              @memcpy(path_expanded_buf[search_path.len + 1 ..][0..file_slice.len], file_slice);
  248              path_expanded_buf[path_len] = 0;
  249              const full_path = path_expanded_buf[0..path_len :0].ptr;
  250              // Replace here, switch on error (any error means that replace
  251              // failed, but we might need to retry).
  252              err = posix.errno(posix.system.execve(full_path, argsZ, envp));
  253              switch (err) {
  254                  .ACCES => seen_eacces = true,
  255                  .NOENT, .NOTDIR => {},
  256                  else => break :execve err,
  257              }
  258          }
  259  
  260          if (seen_eacces) break :execve .ACCES;
  261          break :execve err;
  262      };

  426          null,
  427          windows.TRUE,

  429          if (env_w) |w| w.ptr else null,
  430          if (cwd_w) |w| w.ptr else null,
  431          @ptrCast(&startup_info_ex.StartupInfo),
  432          &process_information,
  433      ) == windows.FALSE) return windows.unexpectedError(windows.GetLastError());

n23 src/os/flatpak.zig:122-139
  122      pub fn spawn(self: *FlatpakHostCommand, alloc: Allocator) !u32 {
  123          const thread = try std.Thread.spawn(.{}, threadMain, .{ self, alloc });
  124          thread.setName(global.io(), "flatpak-host-command") catch {};
  125          // We don't track this thread, it will terminate on its own on command exit
  126          thread.detach();
  127  
  128          // Wait for the process to start or error.
  129          self.state_mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  130          defer self.state_mutex.unlock(global.io());
  131          while (self.state == .init) self.state_cv.waitUncancelable(global.io(), &self.state_mutex);
  132  
  133          return switch (self.state) {
  134              .init => unreachable,
  135              .err => Error.FlatpakSpawnFail,
  136              .started => |v| v.pid,
  137              .exited => |v| v.pid,
  138          };
  139      }

n24 src/terminal/stream.zig:588-604
  588  pub fn Stream(comptime H: type) type {
  589      return struct {
  590          const Self = @This();
  591  
  592          pub const Action = streampkg.Action;
  593          pub const Handler = H;
  594  
  595          const T = switch (@typeInfo(Handler)) {
  596              .pointer => |p| p.child,
  597              else => Handler,
  598          };
  599  
  600          handler: Handler,
  601          parser: Parser,
  602          utf8decoder: UTF8Decoder,
  603          continuation: ?continuationpkg.Tracker,
  604  
n24 src/terminal/stream.zig:643-671
  643          /// This takes ownership of the handler and will call deinit
  644          /// when the stream is deinitialized.
  645          pub fn init(options: Options) Self {
  646              // Initialize the parser
  647              var parser: Parser = .init();
  648              if (options.allocator) |alloc| parser.osc_parser.alloc = alloc;
  649              parser.osc_parser.unknown_max_bytes = options.osc_unknown_max_bytes;
  650  
  651              // Initialize the continuation tracker if one is requested.
  652              var tracker: ?continuationpkg.Tracker = null;
  653              if (options.allocator) |alloc| {
  654                  if (options.continuation_max_bytes) |max_bytes| {
  655                      if (max_bytes > 0) tracker = .init(alloc, max_bytes);
  656                  }
  657              }
  658  
  659              return .{
  660                  .handler = options.handler,
  661                  .parser = parser,
  662                  .utf8decoder = .{},
  663                  .continuation = tracker,
  664              };
  665          }
  666  
  667          pub fn deinit(self: *Self) void {
  668              if (self.continuation) |*tracker| tracker.deinit();
  669              self.parser.deinit();
  670              self.handler.deinit();

n25 src/termio/Termio.zig:705-711
  705  pub fn processOutput(self: *Termio, buf: []const u8) void {
  706      // We are modifying terminal state from here on out and we need
  707      // the lock to grab our read data.
  708      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  709      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  710      self.processOutputLocked(buf);
  711  }
n25 src/termio/Termio.zig:736-754
  736      // If we have an inspector, we enter SLOW MODE because we need to
  737      // process a byte at a time alternating between the inspector handler
  738      // and the termio handler. This is very slow compared to our optimizations
  739      // below but at least users only pay for it if they're using the inspector.
  740      if (self.renderer_state.inspector) |insp| {
  741          for (buf, 0..) |byte, i| {
  742              insp.recordPtyRead(
  743                  self.alloc,
  744                  &self.terminal,
  745                  buf[i .. i + 1],
  746              ) catch |err| {
  747                  log.err("error recording pty read in inspector err={}", .{err});
  748              };
  749  
  750              self.terminal_stream.next(byte);
  751          }
  752      } else {
  753          self.terminal_stream.nextSlice(buf);
  754      }

n25 src/terminal/Terminal.zig:344-404
  344  pub fn init(
  345      io_impl: std.Io,
  346      alloc: Allocator,
  347      opts: Options,
  348  ) !Terminal {
  349      const cols = opts.cols;
  350      const rows = opts.rows;
  351  
  352      // The screens and scrolling region need at least one row and column.
  353      // Check this before allocating so there is nothing to clean up.
  354      if (cols == 0 or rows == 0) return error.InvalidValue;
  355  
  356      var screen_set: ScreenSet = try .init(io_impl, alloc, .{
  357          .cols = cols,
  358          .rows = rows,
  359          .max_scrollback_bytes = opts.max_scrollback_bytes,
  360          .max_scrollback_lines = opts.max_scrollback_lines,
  361          .kitty_image_storage_limit = opts.kitty_image_storage_limit,
  362          .kitty_image_loading_limits = opts.kitty_image_loading_limits,
  363      });
  364      errdefer screen_set.deinit(alloc);
  365  
  366      var result: Terminal = .{
  367          .cols = cols,
  368          .rows = rows,
  369          .screens = screen_set,
  370          .tabstops = try .init(alloc, cols, TABSTOP_INTERVAL),
  371          .scrolling_region = .{
  372              .top = 0,
  373              .bottom = rows - 1,
  374              .left = 0,
  375              .right = cols - 1,
  376          },
  377          .pwd = .empty,
  378          .title = .empty,
  379          .colors = opts.colors,
  380          .modes = .{
  381              .values = opts.default_modes,
  382              .default = opts.default_modes,
  383          },
  384          .cursor = .{
  385              .default_style = opts.default_cursor_style,
  386              .default_blink = opts.default_cursor_blink,
  387          },
  388          .default_xt_checksum = opts.default_xt_checksum,
  389      };
  390      result.flags.xt_checksum = opts.default_xt_checksum;
  391      result.setCursorStyle(.default);
  392      return result;
  393  }
  394  
  395  pub fn deinit(self: *Terminal, alloc: Allocator) void {
  396      self.tabstops.deinit(alloc);
  397      self.screens.deinit(alloc);
  398      self.colors.palette.deinit(alloc);
  399      self.pwd.deinit(alloc);
  400      self.title.deinit(alloc);
  401      self.glyph_glossary.deinit(alloc);
  402      if (self.kitty_dnd) |dnd| dnd.destroy(alloc);
  403      self.* = undefined;
  404  }

  182  pub const DerivedConfig = struct {
  183      arena: ArenaAllocator,
  184  
  185      palette: terminalpkg.color.Palette,
  186      image_storage_limit: usize,
  187      cursor_style: terminalpkg.CursorStyle,
  188      cursor_blink: ?bool,
  189      cursor_color: ?configpkg.Config.TerminalColor,
  190      foreground: configpkg.Config.Color,
  191      background: configpkg.Config.Color,
  192      osc_color_report_format: configpkg.Config.OSCColorReportFormat,
  193      clipboard_write: configpkg.ClipboardAccess,
  194      clipboard_write_limit: usize,
  195      enquiry_response: []const u8,
  196      xt_checksum_report: bool,
  197      xt_checksum_extension: terminalpkg.xt_checksum.Flags,
  198      conditional_state: configpkg.ConditionalState,
  199  
  200      pub fn init(
  201          alloc_gpa: Allocator,
  202          config: *const configpkg.Config,
  203      ) !DerivedConfig {
  204          var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(alloc_gpa);
  205          errdefer arena.deinit();
  206          const alloc = arena.allocator();
  207  
  208          const palette: terminalpkg.color.Palette = palette: {
  209              if (config.@"palette-generate") generate: {
  210                  if (config.palette.mask.findFirstSet() == null) {
  211                      // If the user didn't set any values manually, then
  212                      // we're using the default palette and we don't need
  213                      // to apply the generation code to it.
  214                      break :generate;
  215                  }
  216  
  217                  break :palette terminalpkg.color.generate256Color(config.palette.value, config.palette.mask, config.background.toTerminalRGB(), config.foreground.toTerminalRGB(), config.@"palette-harmonious");
  218              }
  219  
  220              break :palette config.palette.value;
  221          };
  222  
  223          return .{
  224              .palette = palette,
  225              .image_storage_limit = config.@"image-storage-limit",
  226              .cursor_style = config.@"cursor-style",
  227              .cursor_blink = config.@"cursor-style-blink",
  228              .cursor_color = config.@"cursor-color",
  229              .foreground = config.foreground,
  230              .background = config.background,
  231              .osc_color_report_format = config.@"osc-color-report-format",
  232              .clipboard_write = config.@"clipboard-write",
  233              .clipboard_write_limit = config.@"clipboard-write-limit-bytes".value,
  234              .enquiry_response = try alloc.dupe(u8, config.@"enquiry-response"),
  235              .xt_checksum_report = config.@"vt-xt-checksum-report",
  236              .xt_checksum_extension = xtChecksumFlags(config.@"vt-xt-checksum-extension"),
  237              .conditional_state = config._conditional_state,
  238  
  239              // This has to be last so that we copy AFTER the arena allocations
  240              // above happen (Zig assigns in order).
  241              .arena = arena,
  242          };
  243      }
  244  
  245      pub fn deinit(self: *DerivedConfig) void {
  246          self.arena.deinit();
  247      }
  248  };

n26 src/termio/Termio.zig:437-447
  437  pub fn queueMessage(
  438      self: *Termio,
  439      msg: termio.Message,
  440      mutex: MutexState,
  441  ) void {
  442      self.mailbox.send(msg, switch (mutex) {
  443          .locked => self.renderer_state.mutex,
  444          .unlocked => null,
  445      });

n26 src/termio/Termio.zig:466-481
  466  pub fn changeConfig(self: *Termio, td: *ThreadData, config: *DerivedConfig) !void {
  467      // The remainder of this function is modifying terminal state or
  468      // the read thread data, all of which requires holding the renderer
  469      // state lock.
  470      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  471      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  472  
  473      // Deinit our old config. We do this in the lock because the
  474      // stream handler may be referencing the old config (i.e. enquiry resp)
  475      self.config.deinit();
  476      self.config = config.*;
  477  
  478      // Update our stream handler. The stream handler uses the same
  479      // renderer mutex so this is safe to do despite being executed
  480      // from another thread.
  481      self.terminal_stream.handler.changeConfig(&self.config);

n26 src/termio/mailbox.zig:11-55
   11  /// A queue used for storing messages that is periodically drained.
   12  /// Typically used by a multi-threaded application. The capacity is
   13  /// hardcoded to a value that empirically has made sense for Ghostty usage
   14  /// but I'm open to changing it with good arguments.
   15  const Queue = BlockingQueue(termio.Message, 64);
   16  
   17  /// The location to where write-related messages are sent.
   18  pub const Mailbox = union(enum) {
   19      // /// Write messages to an unbounded list backed by an allocator.
   20      // /// This is useful for single-threaded applications where you're not
   21      // /// afraid of running out of memory. You should be careful that you're
   22      // /// processing this in a timely manner though since some heavy workloads
   23      // /// will produce a LOT of messages.
   24      // ///
   25      // /// At the time of authoring this, the primary use case for this is
   26      // /// testing more than anything, but it probably will have a use case
   27      // /// in libghostty eventually.
   28      // unbounded: std.ArrayList(termio.Message),
   29  
   30      /// Write messages to a SPSC queue for multi-threaded applications.
   31      spsc: struct {
   32          queue: *Queue,
   33          wakeup: xev.Async,
   34      },
   35  
   36      /// Init the SPSC writer.
   37      pub fn initSPSC(alloc: Allocator) !Mailbox {
   38          var queue = try Queue.create(alloc);
   39          errdefer queue.destroy(alloc);
   40  
   41          var wakeup = try xev.Async.init();
   42          errdefer wakeup.deinit();
   43  
   44          return .{ .spsc = .{ .queue = queue, .wakeup = wakeup } };
   45      }
   46  
   47      pub fn deinit(self: *Mailbox, alloc: Allocator) void {
   48          switch (self.*) {
   49              .spsc => |*v| {
   50                  while (v.queue.pop(global.io())) |msg| msg.deinit();
   51                  v.queue.destroy(alloc);
   52                  v.wakeup.deinit();
   53              },
   54          }
   55      }
n26 src/termio/Thread.zig:321-326
  321              .change_config => |config| {
  322                  defer config.alloc.destroy(config.ptr);
  323                  try io.changeConfig(data, config.ptr);
  324              },
  325              .inspector => |v| self.flags.has_inspector = v,
  326              .resize => |v| self.handleResize(cb, v),

   25  mailbox: termio.Mailbox,

n27 src/Surface.zig:840-858
  840      // Stop our IO thread
  841      {
  842          self.io_thread.stop.notify() catch |err|
  843              log.err("error notifying io thread to stop, may stall err={}", .{err});
  844          self.io_thr.join();
  845      }
  846  
  847      // We need to deinit AFTER everything is stopped, since there are
  848      // shared values between the two threads.
  849      //
  850      // The renderer must be deinitialized before the renderer thread:
  851      // on macOS the renderer's display link callback points at the

  853      // stops the display link for good.

n27 src/termio/Thread.zig:1-12
    1  //! Represents the "writer" thread for terminal IO. The reader side is
    2  //! handled by the Termio struct itself and dependent on the underlying
    3  //! implementation (i.e. if its a pty, manual, etc.).
    4  //!
    5  //! The writer thread does handle writing bytes to the pty but also handles
    6  //! different events such as starting synchronized output, changing some
    7  //! modes (like linefeed), etc. The goal is to offload as much from the
    8  //! reader thread as possible since it is the hot path in parsing VT
    9  //! sequences and updating terminal state.
   10  //!
   11  //! This thread state can only be used by one thread at a time.
   12  pub const Thread = @This();

n27 src/termio/Thread.zig:89-286
   89  /// Initialize the thread. This does not START the thread. This only sets
   90  /// up all the internal state necessary prior to starting the thread. It
   91  /// is up to the caller to start the thread with the threadMain entrypoint.
   92  pub fn init(
   93      alloc: Allocator,
   94  ) !Thread {
   95      // Create our event loop.
   96      var loop = try xev.Loop.init(.{});
   97      errdefer loop.deinit();
   98  
   99      // This async handle is used to stop the loop and force the thread to end.
  100      var stop_h = try xev.Async.init();
  101      errdefer stop_h.deinit();
  102  
  103      // This timer is used for selection scrolling.
  104      var scroll_h = try xev.Timer.init();
  105      errdefer scroll_h.deinit();
  106  
  107      // This timer is used to coalesce resize events.
  108      var coalesce_h = try xev.Timer.init();
  109      errdefer coalesce_h.deinit();
  110  
  111      // This timer is used to reset synchronized output modes.
  112      var sync_reset_h = try xev.Timer.init();
  113      errdefer sync_reset_h.deinit();
  114  
  115      return Thread{
  116          .alloc = alloc,
  117          .loop = loop,
  118          .stop = stop_h,
  119          .scroll = scroll_h,
  120          .coalesce = coalesce_h,
  121          .sync_reset = sync_reset_h,
  122      };
  123  }
  124  
  125  /// Clean up the thread. This is only safe to call once the thread
  126  /// completes executing; the caller must join prior to this.
  127  pub fn deinit(self: *Thread) void {
  128      self.scroll.deinit();
  129      self.coalesce.deinit();
  130      self.sync_reset.deinit();
  131      self.stop.deinit();
  132      self.loop.deinit();
  133  }
  134  
  135  /// The main entrypoint for the thread.
  136  pub fn threadMain(self: *Thread, io: *termio.Termio) void {
  137      // Call child function so we can use errors...
  138      self.threadMain_(io) catch |err| {
  139          log.warn("error in io thread err={}", .{err});
  140  
  141          // Use an arena to simplify memory management below
  142          var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
  143          defer arena.deinit();
  144          const alloc = arena.allocator();
  145  
  146          // If there is an error, we replace our terminal screen with
  147          // the error message. It might be better in the future to send
  148          // the error to the surface thread and let the apprt deal with it
  149          // in some way but this works for now. Without this, the user would
  150          // just see a blank terminal window.
  151          io.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  152          defer io.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  153          const t = io.renderer_state.terminal;
  154  
  155          // Hide the cursor
  156          t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
  157  
  158          // This is weird but just ensures that no matter what our underlying
  159          // implementation we have the errors below. For example, Windows doesn't
  160          // have "OpenptyFailed".
  161          const Err = @TypeOf(err) || error{
  162              OpenptyFailed,
  163              InputNotFound,
  164              InputFailed,
  165          };
  166  
  167          switch (@as(Err, @errorCast(err))) {
  168              error.OpenptyFailed => {
  169                  const str =
  170                      \\Your system cannot allocate any more pty devices.
  171                      \\
  172                      \\Ghostty requires a pty device to launch a new terminal.
  173                      \\This error is usually due to having too many terminal
  174                      \\windows open or having another program that is using too
  175                      \\many pty devices.
  176                      \\
  177                      \\Please free up some pty devices and try again.
  178                  ;
  179  
  180                  t.eraseDisplay(.complete, false);
  181                  t.printString(str) catch {};
  182              },
  183  
  184              error.InputNotFound,
  185              error.InputFailed,
  186              => {
  187                  const str =
  188                      \\A configured `input` path was not found, was not readable,
  189                      \\was too large, or the underlying pty failed to accept
  190                      \\the write.
  191                      \\
  192                      \\Ghostty can't continue since it can't guarantee that
  193                      \\initial terminal state will be as desired. Please review
  194                      \\the value of `input` in your configuration file and
  195                      \\ensure that all the path values exist and are readable.
  196                  ;
  197  
  198                  t.eraseDisplay(.complete, false);
  199                  t.printString(str) catch {};
  200              },
  201  
  202              else => {
  203                  const str = alloc.print(
  204                      \\error starting IO thread: {}
  205                      \\
  206                      \\The underlying shell or command was unable to be started.
  207                      \\This error is usually due to exhausting a system resource.
  208                      \\If this looks like a bug, please report it.
  209                      \\
  210                      \\This terminal is non-functional. Please close it and try again.
  211                  ,
  212                      .{err},
  213                  ) catch
  214                      \\Out of memory. This terminal is non-functional. Please close it and try again.
  215                  ;
  216  
  217                  t.eraseDisplay(.complete, false);
  218                  t.printString(str) catch {};
  219              },
  220          }
  221      };
  222  
  223      // If our loop is not stopped, then we need to keep running so that
  224      // messages are drained and we can wait for the surface to send a stop
  225      // message.
  226      if (!self.loop.stopped()) {
  227          log.warn("abrupt io thread exit detected, starting xev to drain mailbox", .{});
  228          defer log.debug("io thread fully exiting after abnormal failure", .{});
  229          self.flags.drain = true;
  230          self.loop.run(.until_done) catch |err| {
  231              log.err("failed to start xev loop for draining err={}", .{err});
  232          };
  233      }
  234  }
  235  
  236  fn threadMain_(self: *Thread, io: *termio.Termio) !void {
  237      defer log.debug("IO thread exited", .{});
  238  
  239      // Right now, on Darwin, `std.Thread.setName` can only name the current
  240      // thread, and we have no way to get the current thread from within it,
  241      // so instead we use this code to name the thread instead.
  242      if (builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
  243          internal_os.macos.pthread_setname_np(&"io".*);
  244      }
  245  
  246      // Setup our crash metadata
  247      crash.sentry.thread_state = .{
  248          .type = .io,
  249          .surface = io.surface_mailbox.surface,
  250      };
  251      defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
  252  
  253      // Get the mailbox. This must be an SPSC mailbox for threading.
  254      const mailbox = switch (io.mailbox) {
  255          .spsc => |*v| v,
  256          // else => return error.TermioUnsupportedMailbox,
  257      };
  258  
  259      // This is the data sent to xev callbacks. We want a pointer to both
  260      // ourselves and the thread data so we can thread that through (pun intended).
  261      var cb: CallbackData = .{ .self = self, .io = io };
  262  
  263      // Run our thread start/end callbacks. This allows the implementation
  264      // to hook into the event loop as needed. The thread data is created
  265      // on the stack here so that it has a stable pointer throughout the
  266      // lifetime of the thread.
  267      try io.threadEnter(self, &cb.data);
  268      defer cb.data.deinit();
  269      defer io.threadExit(&cb.data);
  270  
  271      // Start the async handlers.
  272      mailbox.wakeup.wait(&self.loop, &self.wakeup_c, CallbackData, &cb, wakeupCallback);
  273      self.stop.wait(&self.loop, &self.stop_c, CallbackData, &cb, stopCallback);
  274  
  275      // Run
  276      log.debug("starting IO thread", .{});
  277      defer log.debug("starting IO thread shutdown", .{});
  278      try self.loop.run(.until_done);
  279  }
  280  
  281  /// This is the data passed to xev callbacks on the thread.
  282  const CallbackData = struct {
  283      self: *Thread,
  284      io: *termio.Termio,
  285      data: termio.Termio.ThreadData = undefined,
  286  };
n27 src/termio/Thread.zig:473-482
  473  fn stopCallback(
  474      cb_: ?*CallbackData,
  475      _: *xev.Loop,
  476      _: *xev.Completion,
  477      r: xev.Async.WaitError!void,
  478  ) xev.CallbackAction {
  479      _ = r catch unreachable;
  480      cb_.?.self.loop.stop();
  481      return .disarm;
  482  }
n28 src/Surface.zig:899-920
  899  fn queueIo(
  900      self: *Surface,
  901      msg: termio.Message,
  902      mutex: termio.Termio.MutexState,
  903  ) void {
  904      // In readonly mode, we don't allow any writes through to the pty.
  905      if (self.readonly) {
  906          switch (msg) {
  907              .write_small,
  908              .write_stable,
  909              .write_alloc,
  910              => {
  911                  msg.deinit();
  912                  return;
  913              },
  914  
  915              else => {},
  916          }
  917      }
  918  
  919      self.io.queueMessage(msg, mutex);
  920  }

n28 src/termio/mailbox.zig:30-34
   30      /// Write messages to a SPSC queue for multi-threaded applications.
   31      spsc: struct {
   32          queue: *Queue,
   33          wakeup: xev.Async,
   34      },
n28 src/termio/mailbox.zig:63-108
   63      pub fn send(
   64          self: *Mailbox,
   65          msg: termio.Message,
   66          mutex: ?*std.Io.Mutex,
   67      ) void {
   68          switch (self.*) {
   69              .spsc => |*mb| send: {
   70                  // Try to write to the queue with an instant timeout. This is the
   71                  // fast path because we can queue without a lock.
   72                  if (mb.queue.push(global.io(), msg, .{ .instant = {} }) > 0) break :send;
   73  
   74                  // If we enter this conditional, the queue is full. We wake up
   75                  // the writer thread so that it can process messages to clear up
   76                  // space. However, the writer thread may require the renderer
   77                  // lock so we need to unlock.
   78                  mb.wakeup.notify() catch |err| {
   79                      log.warn("failed to wake up writer, data will be dropped err={}", .{err});
   80                      msg.deinit();
   81                      return;
   82                  };
   83  
   84                  // Unlock the renderer state so the writer thread can acquire it.
   85                  // Then try to queue our message before continuing. This is a very
   86                  // slow path because we are having a lot of contention for data.
   87                  // But this only gets triggered in certain pathological cases.
   88                  //
   89                  // Note that writes themselves don't require a lock, but there
   90                  // are other messages in the writer queue (resize, focus) that
   91                  // could acquire the lock. This is why we have to release our lock
   92                  // here.
   93                  if (mutex) |m| m.unlock(global.io());
   94                  defer if (mutex) |m| m.lockUncancelable(global.io());
   95                  if (mb.queue.push(global.io(), msg, .{ .forever = {} }) == 0) msg.deinit();
   96              },
   97          }
   98      }
   99  
  100      /// Notify that there are new messages. This may be a noop depending
  101      /// on the writer type.
  102      pub fn notify(self: *Mailbox) void {
  103          switch (self.*) {
  104              .spsc => |*v| v.wakeup.notify() catch |err| {
  105                  log.warn("failed to notify writer, data will be dropped err={}", .{err});
  106              },
  107          }
  108      }

   29      /// The number of milliseconds to coalesce certain messages like resize for.
   30      /// Not all message types are coalesced.
   31      const min_ms = 25;
   32  
   33      resize: ?renderer.Size = null,
   34  };
