n37 src/renderer/metal/Frame.zig:67-91
   67  fn bufferCompleted(
   68      block: *const CompletionBlock.Context,
   69      buffer_id: objc.c.id,
   70  ) callconv(.c) void {
   71      const buffer = objc.Object.fromId(buffer_id);
   72  
   73      // Get our command buffer status to pass back to the generic renderer.
   74      const status = buffer.getProperty(mtl.MTLCommandBufferStatus, "status");
   75      const health: Health = switch (status) {
   76          .@"error" => .unhealthy,
   77          else => .healthy,
   78      };
   79  
   80      // If the frame is healthy, present it.
   81      if (health == .healthy) {
   82          block.renderer.api.present(
   83              block.target.*,
   84              block.sync,
   85          ) catch |err| {
   86              log.err("Failed to present render target: err={}", .{err});
   87          };
   88      }
   89  
   90      block.renderer.frameCompleted(health);
   91  }
n37 src/renderer/metal/Frame.zig:105-131
  105  /// Complete this frame and present the target.
  106  ///
  107  /// If `sync` is true, this will block until the frame is presented.
  108  pub inline fn complete(self: *Self, sync: bool) void {
  109      // If we don't need to complete synchronously,
  110      // we add our block as a completion handler.
  111      //
  112      // It will be copied when we add the handler, and then the
  113      // copy will be deallocated by the objc runtime on success.
  114      if (!sync) {
  115          self.buffer.msgSend(
  116              void,
  117              objc.sel("addCompletedHandler:"),
  118              .{&self.block},
  119          );
  120      }
  121  
  122      self.buffer.msgSend(void, objc.sel("commit"), .{});
  123  
  124      // If we need to complete synchronously, we wait until
  125      // the buffer is completed and invoke the block directly.
  126      if (sync) {
  127          self.buffer.msgSend(void, "waitUntilCompleted", .{});
  128          self.block.sync = true;
  129          CompletionBlock.invoke(&self.block, .{self.buffer.value});
  130      }
  131  }
n37 src/renderer/opengl/Frame.zig:51-90
   51  /// Complete this frame and present the target.
   52  ///
   53  /// If `sync` is true, this will block until the frame is presented.
   54  ///
   55  /// NOTE: For OpenGL, `sync` is ignored and we never block, instead
   56  /// pushing the newly presented and exported frame to the frame queue.
   57  pub fn complete(self: *const Self, sync: bool) void {
   58      _ = sync;
   59  
   60      // If there are any GL errors, consider the frame unhealthy.
   61      const health: Health = if (gl.errors.getError()) .healthy else |_| .unhealthy;
   62  
   63      // If the frame is healthy, draw it to an ExportedFrame
   64      // Then sync before exporting and pushing to the present queue.
   65      // The apprt pulls from this queue in its snapshot handler.
   66      const presented_frame: ?OpenGL.ExportedFrame = if (health == .healthy) frame: {
   67          const frame = self.renderer.api.present(
   68              self.target.*,
   69              self.renderer.presentationHealth(),
   70          ) catch |err| {
   71              log.warn("failed to present render target: err={}", .{err});
   72              break :frame null;
   73          };
   74          break :frame frame;
   75      } else null;
   76  
   77      // Sync after frame draw AND frame present GL calls.
   78      gl.finish();
   79  
   80      // At this point the ExportedFrame is finished and can be shared
   81      if (presented_frame) |frame| {
   82          self.renderer.pushFrame(frame);
   83  
   84          // Notify the surface that it should redraw
   85          _ = self.renderer.surface_mailbox.push(.redraw, .{ .forever = {} });
   86      }
   87  
   88      // Report the health to the renderer.
   89      self.renderer.frameCompleted(health);
   90  }

  209  /// The search state for the surface.
  210  const Search = struct {
  211      state: terminal.search.Thread,
  212      thread: std.Thread,
  213  
  214      pub fn deinit(self: *Search) void {
  215          // Notify the thread to stop
  216          self.state.stop.notify() catch |err| log.err(
  217              "error notifying search thread to stop, may stall err={}",
  218              .{err},
  219          );
  220  
  221          // Wait for the OS thread to quit
  222          self.thread.join();
  223  
  224          // Now it is safe to deinit the state
  225          self.state.deinit();
  226      }
  227  };

n38 src/Surface.zig:931-956
  931  /// Activate the inspector. This will begin collecting inspection data.
  932  /// This will not affect the GUI. The GUI must use performAction to
  933  /// show/hide the inspector UI.
  934  pub fn activateInspector(self: *Surface) !void {
  935      if (self.inspector != null) return;
  936  
  937      // Setup the inspector
  938      const ptr = try self.alloc.create(inspectorpkg.Inspector);
  939      errdefer self.alloc.destroy(ptr);
  940      ptr.* = try inspectorpkg.Inspector.init(self.alloc);
  941      errdefer ptr.deinit(self.alloc);
  942      self.inspector = ptr;
  943      errdefer self.inspector = null;
  944  
  945      // Put the inspector onto the render state
  946      {
  947          self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  948          defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  949          assert(self.renderer_state.inspector == null);
  950          self.renderer_state.inspector = self.inspector;
  951      }
  952  
  953      // Notify our components we have an inspector active
  954      _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .inspector = true }, .{ .forever = {} });
  955      self.queueIo(.{ .inspector = true }, .unlocked);
  956  }
n38 src/Surface.zig:971-991
  971  /// Deactivate the inspector and stop collecting any information.
  972  pub fn deactivateInspector(self: *Surface) void {
  973      const insp = self.inspector orelse return;
  974  
  975      // Remove the inspector from the render state
  976      {
  977          self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  978          defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  979          assert(self.renderer_state.inspector != null);
  980          self.renderer_state.inspector = null;
  981      }
  982  
  983      // Notify our components we have deactivated inspector
  984      _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .inspector = false }, .{ .forever = {} });
  985      self.queueIo(.{ .inspector = false }, .unlocked);
  986  
  987      // Deinit the inspector
  988      insp.deinit(self.alloc);
  989      self.alloc.destroy(insp);
  990      self.inspector = null;
  991  }

 1485  fn searchCallback(event: terminal.search.Thread.Event, ud: ?*anyopaque) void {
 1486      // IMPORTANT: This function is run on the SEARCH THREAD! It is NOT SAFE
 1487      // to access anything other than values that never change on the surface.
 1488      // The surface is guaranteed to be valid for the lifetime of the search
 1489      // thread.
 1490      const self: *Surface = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ud.?));
 1491      self.searchCallback_(event) catch |err| {
 1492          log.warn("error in search callback err={}", .{err});
 1493      };
 1494  }
 1495  
 1496  fn searchCallback_(
 1497      self: *Surface,
 1498      event: terminal.search.Thread.Event,
 1499  ) !void {
 1500      // NOTE: This runs on the search thread.
 1501  
 1502      switch (event) {
 1503          .viewport_matches => |matches_unowned| {
 1504              var arena: ArenaAllocator = .init(self.alloc);
 1505              errdefer arena.deinit();
 1506              const alloc = arena.allocator();
 1507  
 1508              const matches = try alloc.dupe(terminal.highlight.Flattened, matches_unowned);
 1509              for (matches) |*m| m.* = try m.clone(alloc);
 1510  
 1511              _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
 1512                  global.io(),
 1513                  .{ .search_viewport_matches = .{
 1514                      .arena = arena,
 1515                      .matches = matches,
 1516                  } },
 1517                  .forever,
 1518              );
 1519              try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
 1520          },
 1521  
 1522          .selected_match => |selected_| {
 1523              if (selected_) |sel| {
 1524                  // Copy the flattened match.
 1525                  var arena: ArenaAllocator = .init(self.alloc);
 1526                  errdefer arena.deinit();
 1527                  const alloc = arena.allocator();
 1528                  const match = try sel.highlight.clone(alloc);
 1529  
 1530                  _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
 1531                      global.io(),
 1532                      .{ .search_selected_match = .{
 1533                          .arena = arena,
 1534                          .match = match,
 1535                      } },
 1536                      .forever,
 1537                  );
 1538  
 1539                  // Send the selected index to the surface mailbox
 1540                  _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
 1541                      .{ .search_selected = sel.idx },
 1542                      .forever,
 1543                  );
 1544              } else {
 1545                  // Reset our selected match
 1546                  _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
 1547                      global.io(),
 1548                      .{ .search_selected_match = null },
 1549                      .forever,
 1550                  );
 1551  
 1552                  // Reset the selected index
 1553                  _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
 1554                      .{ .search_selected = null },
 1555                      .forever,
 1556                  );
 1557              }
 1558  
 1559              try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
 1560          },
 1561  
 1562          .total_matches => |total| {
 1563              _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
 1564                  .{ .search_total = total },
 1565                  .forever,
 1566              );
 1567          },
 1568  
 1569          // When we quit, tell our renderer to reset any search state.
 1570          .quit => {
 1571              _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
 1572                  global.io(),
 1573                  .{ .search_selected_match = null },
 1574                  .forever,
 1575              );
 1576              _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
 1577                  global.io(),
 1578                  .{ .search_viewport_matches = .{
 1579                      .arena = .init(self.alloc),
 1580                      .matches = &.{},
 1581                  } },
 1582                  .forever,
 1583              );
 1584              try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
 1585  
 1586              // Reset search totals in the surface
 1587              _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
 1588                  .{ .search_total = null },
 1589                  .forever,
 1590              );
 1591              _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
 1592                  .{ .search_selected = null },
 1593                  .forever,
 1594              );
 1595          },
 1596  
 1597          // Unhandled, so far.
 1598          .complete => {},
 1599      }
 1600  }

n38 src/terminal/search/Thread.zig:1-9
    1  //! Search thread that handles searching a terminal for a string match.
    2  //! This is expected to run on a dedicated thread to try to prevent too much
    3  //! overhead to other terminal read/write operations.
    4  //!
    5  //! The current architecture of search does acquire global locks for accessing
    6  //! terminal data, so there's still added contention, but we do our best to
    7  //! minimize this by trading off memory usage (copying data to minimize lock
    8  //! time).
    9  pub const Thread = @This();

   49  /// this is a blocking queue so if it is full you will get errors (or block).

   61  stop: xev.Async,
   62  stop_c: xev.Completion = .{},

   65  /// we have a stale active area, viewport, screen change, etc. This is

n38 src/terminal/search/Thread.zig:81-254
   81  /// Initialize the thread. This does not START the thread. This only sets
   82  /// up all the internal state necessary prior to starting the thread. It
   83  /// is up to the caller to start the thread with the threadMain entrypoint.
   84  pub fn init(alloc: Allocator, opts: Options) !Thread {
   85      // The mailbox for messaging this thread
   86      var mailbox = try Mailbox.create(alloc);
   87      errdefer mailbox.destroy(alloc);
   88  
   89      // Create our event loop.
   90      var loop = try xev.Loop.init(.{});
   91      errdefer loop.deinit();
   92  
   93      // This async handle is used to "wake up" the renderer and force a render.
   94      var wakeup_h = try xev.Async.init();
   95      errdefer wakeup_h.deinit();
   96  
   97      // This async handle is used to stop the loop and force the thread to end.
   98      var stop_h = try xev.Async.init();
   99      errdefer stop_h.deinit();
  100  
  101      // Refresh timer, see comments.
  102      var refresh_h = try xev.Timer.init();
  103      errdefer refresh_h.deinit();
  104  
  105      return .{
  106          .alloc = alloc,
  107          .mailbox = mailbox,
  108          .loop = loop,
  109          .wakeup = wakeup_h,
  110          .stop = stop_h,
  111          .refresh = refresh_h,
  112          .opts = opts,
  113      };
  114  }
  115  
  116  /// Clean up the thread. This is only safe to call once the thread
  117  /// completes executing; the caller must join prior to this.
  118  pub fn deinit(self: *Thread) void {
  119      self.refresh.deinit();
  120      self.wakeup.deinit();
  121      self.stop.deinit();
  122      self.loop.deinit();
  123      // Nothing can possibly access the mailbox anymore, destroy it.
  124      self.mailbox.destroy(self.alloc);
  125  
  126      if (self.search) |*s| {
  127          self.opts.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  128          defer self.opts.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  129          s.deinit(self.opts.terminal);
  130      }
  131  }
  132  
  133  /// The main entrypoint for the thread.
  134  pub fn threadMain(self: *Thread) void {
  135      // Call child function so we can use errors...
  136      self.threadMain_() catch |err| {
  137          // In the future, we should expose this on the thread struct.
  138          log.warn("search thread err={}", .{err});
  139      };
  140  }
  141  
  142  fn threadMain_(self: *Thread) !void {
  143      defer log.debug("search thread exited", .{});
  144  
  145      // Right now, on Darwin, `std.Thread.setName` can only name the current
  146      // thread, and we have no way to get the current thread from within it,
  147      // so instead we use this code to name the thread instead.
  148      if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
  149          internal_os.macos.pthread_setname_np(&"search".*);
  150  
  151          // We can run with lower priority than other threads.
  152          const class: internal_os.macos.QosClass = .utility;
  153          if (internal_os.macos.setQosClass(class)) {
  154              log.debug("thread QoS class set class={}", .{class});
  155          } else |err| {
  156              log.warn("error setting QoS class err={}", .{err});
  157          }
  158      }
  159  
  160      // Start the async handlers
  161      self.wakeup.wait(&self.loop, &self.wakeup_c, Thread, self, wakeupCallback);
  162      self.stop.wait(&self.loop, &self.stop_c, Thread, self, stopCallback);
  163  
  164      // Send an initial wakeup so we drain our mailbox immediately.
  165      try self.wakeup.notify();
  166  
  167      // Start the refresh timer
  168      self.startRefreshTimer();
  169  
  170      // Run
  171      log.debug("starting search thread", .{});
  172      defer {
  173          log.debug("starting search thread shutdown", .{});
  174  
  175          // Send the quit message
  176          if (self.opts.event_cb) |cb| {
  177              cb(.quit, self.opts.event_userdata);
  178          }
  179      }
  180  
  181      // Unlike some of our other threads, we interleave search work
  182      // with our xev loop so that we can try to make forward search progress
  183      // while also listening for messages.
  184      while (true) {
  185          // If our loop is canceled then we drain our messages and quit.
  186          if (self.loop.stopped()) {
  187              while (self.mailbox.pop(global.io())) |message| {
  188                  log.debug("mailbox message ignored during shutdown={}", .{message});
  189              }
  190  
  191              return;
  192          }
  193  
  194          const s: *TerminalSearch = if (self.search) |*s| s else {
  195              // If we're not actively searching, we can block the loop
  196              // until it does some work.
  197              try self.loop.run(.once);
  198              continue;
  199          };
  200  
  201          // If we have an active search, we always send any pending
  202          // notifications. Even if the search is complete, there may be
  203          // notifications to send.
  204          if (self.opts.event_cb) |cb| {
  205              self.notify(s, cb, self.opts.event_userdata);
  206          }
  207  
  208          if (s.isComplete()) {
  209              // If our search is complete, there's no more work to do, we
  210              // can block until we have an xev action.
  211              try self.loop.run(.once);
  212              continue;
  213          }
  214  
  215          // Tick the search. This will trigger any event callbacks, lock
  216          // for data loading, etc.
  217          switch (s.tick()) {
  218              // We're complete now when we were not before. Notify!
  219              .complete => {},
  220  
  221              // Forward progress was made.
  222              .progress => {},
  223  
  224              // All searches are blocked. Let's grab the lock and feed data.
  225              .blocked => {
  226                  self.opts.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  227                  defer self.opts.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  228                  self.feedLocked(s);
  229              },
  230          }
  231  
  232          // We have an active search, so we only want to process messages
  233          // we have but otherwise return immediately so we can continue the
  234          // search. If the above completed the search, we still want to
  235          // go around the loop as quickly as possible to send notifications,
  236          // and then we'll block on the loop next time.
  237          try self.loop.run(.no_wait);
  238      }
  239  }
  240  
  241  /// Feed the search from the terminal state. The terminal mutex must
  242  /// be held by the caller.
  243  fn feedLocked(self: *Thread, s: *TerminalSearch) void {
  244      const t = self.opts.terminal;
  245  
  246      // See the `search_viewport_dirty` flag on the terminal to know
  247      // what exactly this is for. But, if this is set, we know the renderer
  248      // found the viewport/active area dirty, so the active area must be
  249      // re-scanned.
  250      const active_dirty = t.flags.search_viewport_dirty;
  251      t.flags.search_viewport_dirty = false;
  252  
  253      s.feed(t, active_dirty);
  254  }
n38 src/terminal/search/Thread.zig:256-329
  256  /// Drain the mailbox.
  257  fn drainMailbox(self: *Thread) !void {
  258      while (self.mailbox.pop(global.io())) |message| {
  259          log.debug("mailbox message={}", .{message});
  260          switch (message) {
  261              .change_needle => |v| {
  262                  defer v.deinit();
  263                  try self.changeNeedle(v.slice());
  264              },
  265              .select => |v| try self.select(v),
  266          }
  267      }
  268  }
  269  
  270  fn select(self: *Thread, sel: ScreenSearch.Select) !void {
  271      const s = if (self.search) |*s| s else return;
  272  
  273      self.opts.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  274      defer self.opts.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  275  
  276      if (try s.select(self.opts.terminal, sel, .if_needed)) {
  277          // No matter what we reset our selected match cache. This will
  278          // trigger a callback which will trigger the renderer to wake up
  279          // so it can be notified the screen scrolled.
  280          self.notify_state.selected = null;
  281      }
  282  }
  283  
  284  /// Change the search term to the given value.
  285  fn changeNeedle(self: *Thread, needle: []const u8) !void {
  286      log.debug("changing search needle to '{s}'", .{needle});
  287  
  288      // Stop the previous search
  289      if (self.search) |*s| {
  290          // If our search is unchanged, do nothing.
  291          if (std.ascii.eqlIgnoreCase(s.needle(), needle)) return;
  292  
  293          {
  294              self.opts.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  295              defer self.opts.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  296              s.deinit(self.opts.terminal);
  297          }
  298          self.search = null;
  299          self.notify_state = .{};
  300  
  301          // When the search changes then we need to emit that it stopped.
  302          if (self.opts.event_cb) |cb| {
  303              cb(
  304                  .{ .total_matches = 0 },
  305                  self.opts.event_userdata,
  306              );
  307              cb(
  308                  .{ .selected_match = null },
  309                  self.opts.event_userdata,
  310              );
  311              cb(
  312                  .{ .viewport_matches = &.{} },
  313                  self.opts.event_userdata,
  314              );
  315          }
  316      }
  317  
  318      // No needle means stop the search.
  319      if (needle.len == 0) return;
  320  
  321      // Setup our search state.
  322      self.search = try .init(self.alloc, needle);
  323      self.notify_state = .{};
  324  
  325      // We need to grab the terminal lock and do an initial feed.
  326      self.opts.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
  327      defer self.opts.mutex.unlock(global.io());
  328      self.feedLocked(&self.search.?);
  329  }
n38 src/terminal/search/Thread.zig:335-415
  335  fn notify(
  336      self: *Thread,
  337      s: *TerminalSearch,
  338      cb: EventCallback,
  339      ud: ?*anyopaque,
  340  ) void {
  341      const state = &self.notify_state;
  342  
  343      // A screen switch makes all previously notified per-screen state
  344      // stale, so reset it to force recalculations and notifications.
  345      if (state.key != s.active_key) {
  346          state.key = s.active_key;
  347          state.total = null;
  348          state.selected = null;
  349      }
  350  
  351      const screen_search = s.activeScreenSearch() orelse return;
  352  
  353      // Check our total match data
  354      const total = screen_search.matchesLen();
  355      if (total != state.total) {
  356          log.debug("notifying total matches={}", .{total});
  357          state.total = total;
  358          cb(.{ .total_matches = total }, ud);
  359      }
  360  
  361      // Check our viewport matches. If they're stale, we collect them
  362      // now. We do this as part of notify and not tick because the
  363      // viewport search is very fast and doesn't require ticked progress
  364      // or feeds.
  365      if (s.stale_viewport_matches) viewport: {
  366          const matches = s.viewportMatches() catch |err| {
  367              log.warn("error collecting viewport matches err={}", .{err});
  368              break :viewport;
  369          };
  370  
  371          log.debug("notifying viewport matches len={}", .{matches.len});
  372          cb(.{ .viewport_matches = matches }, ud);
  373      }
  374  
  375      // Check our last selected match data.
  376      if (screen_search.selected) |m| match: {
  377          const flattened = screen_search.selectedMatch() orelse break :match;
  378          const untracked = flattened.untracked();
  379          if (state.selected) |prev| {
  380              if (prev.idx == m.idx and prev.highlight.eql(untracked)) {
  381                  // Same selection, don't update it.
  382                  break :match;
  383              }
  384          }
  385  
  386          // New selection, notify!
  387          state.selected = .{
  388              .idx = m.idx,
  389              .highlight = untracked,
  390          };
  391  
  392          log.debug("notifying selection updated idx={}", .{m.idx});
  393          cb(
  394              .{ .selected_match = .{
  395                  .idx = m.idx,
  396                  .highlight = flattened,
  397              } },
  398              ud,
  399          );
  400      } else if (state.selected != null) {
  401          log.debug("notifying selection cleared", .{});
  402          state.selected = null;
  403          cb(
  404              .{ .selected_match = null },
  405              ud,
  406          );
  407      }
  408  
  409      // Send our complete notification if we just completed.
  410      if (!state.complete and s.isComplete()) {
  411          log.debug("notifying search complete", .{});
  412          state.complete = true;
  413          cb(.complete, ud);
  414      }
  415  }
n38 src/terminal/search/Thread.zig:441-471
  441  fn wakeupCallback(
  442      self_: ?*Thread,
  443      _: *xev.Loop,
  444      _: *xev.Completion,
  445      r: xev.Async.WaitError!void,
  446  ) xev.CallbackAction {
  447      _ = r catch |err| {
  448          log.warn("error in wakeup err={}", .{err});
  449          return .rearm;
  450      };
  451  
  452      const self = self_.?;
  453  
  454      // When we wake up, we drain the mailbox. Mailbox producers should
  455      // wake up our thread after publishing.
  456      self.drainMailbox() catch |err|
  457          log.warn("error draining mailbox err={}", .{err});
  458  
  459      return .rearm;
  460  }
  461  
  462  fn stopCallback(
  463      self_: ?*Thread,
  464      _: *xev.Loop,
  465      _: *xev.Completion,
  466      r: xev.Async.WaitError!void,
  467  ) xev.CallbackAction {
  468      _ = r catch unreachable;
  469      self_.?.loop.stop();
  470      return .disarm;

n38 src/termio/Termio.zig:736-754
  736      // If we have an inspector, we enter SLOW MODE because we need to
  737      // process a byte at a time alternating between the inspector handler
  738      // and the termio handler. This is very slow compared to our optimizations
  739      // below but at least users only pay for it if they're using the inspector.
  740      if (self.renderer_state.inspector) |insp| {
  741          for (buf, 0..) |byte, i| {
  742              insp.recordPtyRead(
  743                  self.alloc,
  744                  &self.terminal,
  745                  buf[i .. i + 1],
  746              ) catch |err| {
  747                  log.err("error recording pty read in inspector err={}", .{err});
  748              };
  749  
  750              self.terminal_stream.next(byte);
  751          }
  752      } else {
  753          self.terminal_stream.nextSlice(buf);
  754      }

n38 src/inspector/Inspector.zig:74-110
   74  /// Record a keyboard event.
   75  pub fn recordKeyEvent(
   76      self: *Inspector,
   77      alloc: Allocator,
   78      ev: inspector.KeyEvent,
   79  ) Allocator.Error!void {
   80      const max_capacity = 50;
   81  
   82      const events: *widgets.key.EventRing = &self.gui.key_stream.events;
   83      events.append(ev) catch |err| switch (err) {
   84          error.OutOfMemory => if (events.capacity() < max_capacity) {
   85              // We're out of memory, but we can allocate to our capacity.
   86              const new_capacity = @min(events.capacity() * 2, max_capacity);
   87              try events.resize(alloc, new_capacity);
   88              try events.append(ev);
   89          } else {
   90              var it = events.iterator(.forward);
   91              if (it.next()) |old_ev| old_ev.deinit(alloc);
   92              events.deleteOldest(1);
   93              try events.append(ev);
   94          },
   95      };
   96  }
   97

  100      self: *Inspector,
  101      alloc: Allocator,
  102      t: *terminal.Terminal,
  103      data: []const u8,
  104  ) !void {
  105      try self.gui.vt_stream.recordPtyRead(
  106          alloc,
  107          t,
  108          data,
  109      );
  110  }

  651      pub fn initInspector(self: *Surface) !*Inspector {
  652          if (self.inspector) |v| return v;

  655          const inspector = try alloc.create(Inspector);
  656          errdefer alloc.destroy(inspector);
  657          inspector.* = try .init(self);
  658          self.inspector = inspector;
  659          return inspector;
  660      }

  663          if (self.inspector) |v| {
  664              v.deinit();
  665              self.app.core_app.alloc.destroy(v);
  666              self.inspector = null;
  667          }
  668      }

n38 src/apprt/gtk/class/inspector_widget.zig:123-172
  123  
  124      pub fn setSurface(self: *Self, surface_: ?*Surface) void {
  125          const priv = self.private();
  126  
  127          // Do nothing if we're not changing the value.
  128          if (surface_ == priv.surface) return;
  129  
  130          // Setup our notification to happen at the end because we're
  131          // changing values no matter what.
  132          self.as(gobject.Object).freezeNotify();
  133          defer self.as(gobject.Object).thawNotify();
  134          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.surface.impl.param_spec);
  135  
  136          // Deactivate the inspector on the old surface if it exists
  137          // and set our value to null.
  138          if (priv.surface) |old| old: {
  139              priv.surface = null;
  140  
  141              // Remove our weak ref
  142              old.as(gobject.Object).weakUnref(
  143                  surfaceWeakNotify,
  144                  self,
  145              );
  146  
  147              // Deactivate the inspector
  148              const core_surface = old.core() orelse break :old;
  149              core_surface.deactivateInspector();
  150          }
  151  
  152          // Activate the inspector on the new surface.
  153          const surface = surface_ orelse return;
  154          const core_surface = surface.core() orelse return;
  155          core_surface.activateInspector() catch |err| {
  156              log.warn("failed to activate inspector err={}", .{err});
  157              return;
  158          };
  159  
  160          // We use a weak reference on surface to determine if the surface
  161          // was closed while our inspector was active.
  162          surface.as(gobject.Object).weakRef(
  163              surfaceWeakNotify,
  164              self,
  165          );
  166  
  167          // Store our surface. We don't need to ref this because we setup
  168          // the weak notify above.
  169          priv.surface = surface;
  170  
  171          self.queueRender();
  172      }
n38 src/apprt/gtk/class/inspector_widget.zig:177-196
  177      fn surfaceWeakNotify(
  178          ud: ?*anyopaque,
  179          surface: *gobject.Object,
  180      ) callconv(.c) void {
  181          const self: *Self = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ud orelse return));
  182          const priv = self.private();
  183  
  184          // The weak notify docs call out that we can specifically use the
  185          // pointer values for comparison, but the objects themselves are unsafe.
  186          if (@intFromPtr(priv.surface) != @intFromPtr(surface)) return;
  187  
  188          // According to weak notify docs, "surface" is in the "dispose" state.
  189          // Our surface doesn't clear the core surface until the "finalize"
  190          // state so we should be able to safely access it here. We need to
  191          // be really careful though.
  192          const old = priv.surface orelse return;
  193          const core_surface = old.core() orelse return;
  194          core_surface.deactivateInspector();
  195          priv.surface = null;
  196          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.surface.impl.param_spec);

  157  child_exited: bool = false,

 1017  pub fn handleMessage(self: *Surface, msg: Message) !void {
 1018      switch (msg) {
n39 src/Surface.zig:1141-1141

n39 src/Surface.zig:1297-1454
 1297  fn childExited(self: *Surface, info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited) void {
 1298      // Mark our flag that we exited immediately
 1299      self.child_exited = true;
 1300  
 1301      // If our runtime was below some threshold then we assume that this
 1302      // was an abnormal exit and we show an error message.
 1303      if (info.runtime_ms <= self.config.abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms) runtime: {
 1304          // On macOS, our exit code detection doesn't work, possibly
 1305          // because of our `login` wrapper. More investigation required.
 1306          if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
 1307              // If the exit code is 0 then it was a good exit.
 1308              if (info.exit_code == 0) break :runtime;
 1309          }
 1310  
 1311          log.warn("abnormal process exit detected, showing error message", .{});
 1312  
 1313          // Try and show a GUI message. If it returns true, don't do anything else.
 1314          if (self.rt_app.performAction(
 1315              .{ .surface = self },
 1316              .show_child_exited,
 1317              info,
 1318          ) catch |err| gui: {
 1319              log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
 1320              break :gui false;
 1321          }) return;
 1322  
 1323          // If a native GUI notification was not shown, update our terminal to
 1324          // note the abnormal exit.
 1325          self.childExitedAbnormally(info) catch |err| {
 1326              log.err("error handling abnormal child exit err={}", .{err});
 1327              return;
 1328          };
 1329  
 1330          return;
 1331      }
 1332  
 1333      // We output a message so that the user knows what's going on and
 1334      // doesn't think their terminal just froze. We show this unconditionally
 1335      // on close even if `wait_after_command` is false and the surface closes
 1336      // immediately because if a user does an `undo` to restore a closed
 1337      // surface then they will see this message and know the process has
 1338      // completed.
 1339      terminal: {
 1340          // First try and show a native GUI message.
 1341          if (self.rt_app.performAction(
 1342              .{ .surface = self },
 1343              .show_child_exited,
 1344              info,
 1345          ) catch |err| gui: {
 1346              log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
 1347              break :gui false;
 1348          }) break :terminal;
 1349  
 1350          // If the native GUI can't be shown, display a text message in the
 1351          // terminal.
 1352          self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1353          defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1354          const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
 1355          t.carriageReturn();
 1356          t.linefeed() catch break :terminal;
 1357          t.printString("Process exited. Press any key to close the terminal.") catch
 1358              break :terminal;
 1359          t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
 1360  
 1361          // We also want to ensure that normal keyboard encoding is on
 1362          // so that we can close the terminal. We close the terminal on
 1363          // any key press that encodes a character.
 1364          t.modes.set(.disable_keyboard, false);
 1365          t.screens.active.kitty_keyboard.set(.set, .disabled);
 1366      }
 1367  
 1368      // Waiting after command we stop here. The terminal is updated, our
 1369      // state is updated, and now its up to the user to decide what to do.
 1370      if (self.config.wait_after_command) return;
 1371  
 1372      // If we aren't waiting after the command, then we exit immediately
 1373      // with no confirmation.
 1374      self.close();
 1375  }
 1376  
 1377  /// Called when the child process exited abnormally.
 1378  fn childExitedAbnormally(
 1379      self: *Surface,
 1380      info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited,
 1381  ) !void {
 1382      var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
 1383      defer arena.deinit();
 1384      const alloc = arena.allocator();
 1385  
 1386      // Build up our command for the error message
 1387      const command = try std.mem.join(alloc, " ", switch (self.io.backend) {
 1388          .exec => |*exec| exec.subprocess.args,
 1389      });
 1390      const runtime_str = try alloc.print("{d} ms", .{info.runtime_ms});
 1391  
 1392      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1393      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1394      const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
 1395  
 1396      // No matter what move the cursor back to the column 0.
 1397      t.carriageReturn();
 1398  
 1399      // Reset styles
 1400      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1401  
 1402      // If there is data in the viewport, we want to scroll down
 1403      // a little bit and write a horizontal rule before writing
 1404      // our message. This lets the use see the error message the
 1405      // command may have output.
 1406      const viewport_str = try t.plainString(alloc);
 1407      if (viewport_str.len > 0) {
 1408          try t.linefeed();
 1409          for (0..t.cols) |_| try t.print(0x2501);
 1410          t.carriageReturn();
 1411          try t.linefeed();
 1412          try t.linefeed();
 1413      }
 1414  
 1415      // Output our error message
 1416      try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .bright_red });
 1417      try t.setAttribute(.{ .bold = {} });
 1418      try t.printString("Ghostty failed to launch the requested command:");
 1419      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1420  
 1421      t.carriageReturn();
 1422      try t.linefeed();
 1423      try t.linefeed();
 1424      try t.printString(command);
 1425      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1426  
 1427      t.carriageReturn();
 1428      try t.linefeed();
 1429      try t.linefeed();
 1430      try t.printString("Runtime: ");
 1431      try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
 1432      try t.printString(runtime_str);
 1433      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1434  
 1435      // We don't print this on macOS because the exit code is always 0
 1436      // due to the way we launch the process.
 1437      if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
 1438          const exit_code_str = try alloc.print("{d}", .{info.exit_code});
 1439          t.carriageReturn();
 1440          try t.linefeed();
 1441          try t.printString("Exit Code: ");
 1442          try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
 1443          try t.printString(exit_code_str);
 1444          try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1445      }
 1446  
 1447      t.carriageReturn();
 1448      try t.linefeed();
 1449      try t.linefeed();
 1450      try t.printString("Press any key to close the window.");
 1451  
 1452      // Hide the cursor
 1453      t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
 1454  }

n39 src/termio/Exec.zig:274-315
  274  fn processExitCommon(td: *termio.Termio.ThreadData, exit_code: u32) void {
  275      assert(td.backend == .exec);
  276      const execdata = &td.backend.exec;
  277      execdata.exited = true;
  278  
  279      // Determine how long the process was running for.
  280      const runtime_ms: u64 = @max(
  281          0,
  282          execdata.start.untilNow(global.io(), .awake).toMilliseconds(),
  283      );
  284      log.debug("child process exited status={} runtime={}ms", .{ exit_code, runtime_ms });
  285  
  286      // We always notify the surface immediately that the child has
  287      // exited and some metadata about the exit.
  288      _ = td.surface_mailbox.push(.{
  289          .child_exited = .{
  290              .exit_code = exit_code,
  291              .runtime_ms = runtime_ms,
  292          },
  293      }, .{ .forever = {} });
  294  }
  295  
  296  fn processExit(
  297      td_: ?*termio.Termio.ThreadData,
  298      _: *xev.Loop,
  299      _: *xev.Completion,
  300      r: xev.Process.WaitError!u32,
  301  ) xev.CallbackAction {
  302      const exit_code = r catch unreachable;
  303      processExitCommon(td_.?, exit_code);
  304      return .disarm;
  305  }
  306  
  307  fn flatpakExit(
  308      td_: ?*termio.Termio.ThreadData,
  309      _: *xev.Loop,
  310      _: *FlatpakHostCommand.Completion,
  311      r: FlatpakHostCommand.WaitError!u8,
  312  ) void {
  313      const exit_code = r catch unreachable;
  314      processExitCommon(td_.?, exit_code);
  315  }

n39 src/App.zig:273-293
  273  fn drainMailbox(self: *App, rt_app: *apprt.App) !void {
  274      while (self.mailbox.pop(global.io())) |message| {
  275          if (comptime std.log.logEnabled(.debug, .app)) {
  276              switch (message) {
  277                  // these tend to be way too verbose for normal debugging
  278                  else => log.debug("mailbox message={t}", .{message}),
  279              }
  280          }
  281          switch (message) {
  282              .open_config => |v| try self.performAction(
  283                  rt_app,
  284                  .{
  285                      .open_config = switch (v) {
  286                          .os_open => .os_open,
  287                          .new_window => .new_window,
  288                      },
  289                  },
  290              ),
  291              .new_window => |msg| try self.newWindow(rt_app, msg),
  292              .close => |surface| self.closeSurface(surface),
  293              .surface_message => |msg| try self.surfaceMessage(msg.surface, msg.message),
n39 src/App.zig:532-550
  532  fn surfaceMessage(self: *App, surface: *Surface, msg: apprt.surface.Message) !void {
  533      // We want to ensure our window is still active. Window messages
  534      // are quite rare and we normally don't have many windows so we do
  535      // a simple linear search here.
  536      if (self.hasSurface(surface)) {
  537          try surface.handleMessage(msg);
  538      }
  539  
  540      // Window was not found, it probably quit before we handled the message.
  541      // Not a problem.
  542  }
  543  
  544  fn hasSurface(self: *const App, surface: *const Surface) bool {
  545      for (self.surfaces.items) |v| {
  546          if (v.core() == surface) return true;
  547      }
  548  
  549      return false;
  550  }

  603  
  604      /// Send a message to the surface.
  605      pub fn push(self: Mailbox, msg: Message, timeout: Queue.Timeout) Queue.Size {
  606          const result = self.mailbox.push(global.io(), msg, timeout);
  607  
  608          // Wake up our app loop
  609          self.rt_app.wakeup();
  610  
  611          return result;
  612      }
  613  };

   77              return;
   78          };

   97              // Not localized on purpose.

   99              return;
  100          };

  102              banner.as(adw.Banner).setTitle(i18n._("Command Succeeded"));

  104              self.as(gtk.Widget).removeCssClass("abnormal");

  107              self.as(gtk.Widget).removeCssClass("normal");
  108              self.as(gtk.Widget).addCssClass("abnormal");
  109          }
  110      }

 1858          ) -> Bool {

 1869              default:

n40 src/Surface.zig:309-309
  309      abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms: u32,
n40 src/Surface.zig:390-390
  390              .abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms = config.@"abnormal-command-exit-runtime",
n40 src/Surface.zig:1297-1331
 1297  fn childExited(self: *Surface, info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited) void {
 1298      // Mark our flag that we exited immediately
 1299      self.child_exited = true;
 1300  
 1301      // If our runtime was below some threshold then we assume that this
 1302      // was an abnormal exit and we show an error message.
 1303      if (info.runtime_ms <= self.config.abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms) runtime: {
 1304          // On macOS, our exit code detection doesn't work, possibly
 1305          // because of our `login` wrapper. More investigation required.
 1306          if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
 1307              // If the exit code is 0 then it was a good exit.
 1308              if (info.exit_code == 0) break :runtime;
 1309          }
 1310  
 1311          log.warn("abnormal process exit detected, showing error message", .{});
 1312  
 1313          // Try and show a GUI message. If it returns true, don't do anything else.
 1314          if (self.rt_app.performAction(
 1315              .{ .surface = self },
 1316              .show_child_exited,
 1317              info,
 1318          ) catch |err| gui: {
 1319              log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
 1320              break :gui false;
 1321          }) return;
 1322  
 1323          // If a native GUI notification was not shown, update our terminal to
 1324          // note the abnormal exit.
 1325          self.childExitedAbnormally(info) catch |err| {
 1326              log.err("error handling abnormal child exit err={}", .{err});
 1327              return;
 1328          };
 1329  
 1330          return;
 1331      }
n40 src/Surface.zig:1378-1454
 1378  fn childExitedAbnormally(
 1379      self: *Surface,
 1380      info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited,
 1381  ) !void {
 1382      var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
 1383      defer arena.deinit();
 1384      const alloc = arena.allocator();
 1385  
 1386      // Build up our command for the error message
 1387      const command = try std.mem.join(alloc, " ", switch (self.io.backend) {
 1388          .exec => |*exec| exec.subprocess.args,
 1389      });
 1390      const runtime_str = try alloc.print("{d} ms", .{info.runtime_ms});
 1391  
 1392      self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1393      defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1394      const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
 1395  
 1396      // No matter what move the cursor back to the column 0.
 1397      t.carriageReturn();
 1398  
 1399      // Reset styles
 1400      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1401  
 1402      // If there is data in the viewport, we want to scroll down
 1403      // a little bit and write a horizontal rule before writing
 1404      // our message. This lets the use see the error message the
 1405      // command may have output.
 1406      const viewport_str = try t.plainString(alloc);
 1407      if (viewport_str.len > 0) {
 1408          try t.linefeed();
 1409          for (0..t.cols) |_| try t.print(0x2501);
 1410          t.carriageReturn();
 1411          try t.linefeed();
 1412          try t.linefeed();
 1413      }
 1414  
 1415      // Output our error message
 1416      try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .bright_red });
 1417      try t.setAttribute(.{ .bold = {} });
 1418      try t.printString("Ghostty failed to launch the requested command:");
 1419      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1420  
 1421      t.carriageReturn();
 1422      try t.linefeed();
 1423      try t.linefeed();
 1424      try t.printString(command);
 1425      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1426  
 1427      t.carriageReturn();
 1428      try t.linefeed();
 1429      try t.linefeed();
 1430      try t.printString("Runtime: ");
 1431      try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
 1432      try t.printString(runtime_str);
 1433      try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1434  
 1435      // We don't print this on macOS because the exit code is always 0
 1436      // due to the way we launch the process.
 1437      if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
 1438          const exit_code_str = try alloc.print("{d}", .{info.exit_code});
 1439          t.carriageReturn();
 1440          try t.linefeed();
 1441          try t.printString("Exit Code: ");
 1442          try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
 1443          try t.printString(exit_code_str);
 1444          try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
 1445      }
 1446  
 1447      t.carriageReturn();
 1448      try t.linefeed();
 1449      try t.linefeed();
 1450      try t.printString("Press any key to close the window.");
 1451  
 1452      // Hide the cursor
 1453      t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
 1454  }
n40 src/config/Config.zig:1356-1363
 1356  /// The number of milliseconds of runtime below which we consider a process exit
 1357  /// to be abnormal. This is used to show an error message when the process exits
 1358  /// too quickly.
 1359  ///
 1360  /// On Linux, this must be paired with a non-zero exit code. On macOS, we allow
 1361  /// any exit code because of the way shell processes are launched via the login
 1362  /// command.
 1363  @"abnormal-command-exit-runtime": u32 = 250,
n40 src/termio/Exec.zig:274-294
  274  fn processExitCommon(td: *termio.Termio.ThreadData, exit_code: u32) void {
  275      assert(td.backend == .exec);
  276      const execdata = &td.backend.exec;
  277      execdata.exited = true;
  278  
  279      // Determine how long the process was running for.
  280      const runtime_ms: u64 = @max(
  281          0,
  282          execdata.start.untilNow(global.io(), .awake).toMilliseconds(),
  283      );
  284      log.debug("child process exited status={} runtime={}ms", .{ exit_code, runtime_ms });
  285  
  286      // We always notify the surface immediately that the child has
  287      // exited and some metadata about the exit.
  288      _ = td.surface_mailbox.push(.{
  289          .child_exited = .{
  290              .exit_code = exit_code,
  291              .runtime_ms = runtime_ms,
  292          },
  293      }, .{ .forever = {} });
  294  }

  883  pub fn close(self: *Surface) void {

n41 src/Surface.zig:995-1013
  995  pub fn needsConfirmQuit(self: *Surface) bool {
  996      // If the surface is in read-only mode, always require confirmation
  997      if (self.readonly) return true;
  998  
  999      // If the child has exited, then our process is certainly not alive.
 1000      // We check this first to avoid the locking overhead below.
 1001      if (self.child_exited) return false;
 1002  
 1003      // Check the configuration for confirming close behavior.
 1004      return switch (self.config.confirm_close_surface) {
 1005          .always => true,
 1006          .false => false,
 1007          .true => true: {
 1008              self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1009              defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1010              break :true !self.io.terminal.cursorIsAtPrompt();
 1011          },
 1012      };
 1013  }
n41 src/Surface.zig:1297-1297
 1297  fn childExited(self: *Surface, info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited) void {
n41 src/Surface.zig:1333-1375
 1333      // We output a message so that the user knows what's going on and
 1334      // doesn't think their terminal just froze. We show this unconditionally
 1335      // on close even if `wait_after_command` is false and the surface closes
 1336      // immediately because if a user does an `undo` to restore a closed
 1337      // surface then they will see this message and know the process has
 1338      // completed.
 1339      terminal: {
 1340          // First try and show a native GUI message.
 1341          if (self.rt_app.performAction(
 1342              .{ .surface = self },
 1343              .show_child_exited,
 1344              info,
 1345          ) catch |err| gui: {
 1346              log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
 1347              break :gui false;
 1348          }) break :terminal;
 1349  
 1350          // If the native GUI can't be shown, display a text message in the
 1351          // terminal.
 1352          self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1353          defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1354          const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
 1355          t.carriageReturn();
 1356          t.linefeed() catch break :terminal;
 1357          t.printString("Process exited. Press any key to close the terminal.") catch
 1358              break :terminal;
 1359          t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
 1360  
 1361          // We also want to ensure that normal keyboard encoding is on
 1362          // so that we can close the terminal. We close the terminal on
 1363          // any key press that encodes a character.
 1364          t.modes.set(.disable_keyboard, false);
 1365          t.screens.active.kitty_keyboard.set(.set, .disabled);
 1366      }
 1367  
 1368      // Waiting after command we stop here. The terminal is updated, our
 1369      // state is updated, and now its up to the user to decide what to do.
 1370      if (self.config.wait_after_command) return;
 1371  
 1372      // If we aren't waiting after the command, then we exit immediately
 1373      // with no confirmation.
 1374      self.close();
 1375  }
n41 src/Surface.zig:2938-2951
 2938      // Encode and send our key. If we didn't encode anything, then we
 2939      // return the effect as ignored.
 2940      if (try self.encodeKey(
 2941          event,
 2942          if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
 2943      )) |write_req| {
 2944          // If our process is exited and we press a key that results in
 2945          // an encoded value, we close the surface. We want to eventually
 2946          // move this behavior to the apprt probably.
 2947          if (self.child_exited) {
 2948              write_req.deinit();
 2949              self.close();
 2950              return .closed;
 2951          }

n42 src/Surface.zig:993-1013
  993  /// True if the surface requires confirmation to quit. This should be called
  994  /// by apprt to determine if the surface should confirm before quitting.
  995  pub fn needsConfirmQuit(self: *Surface) bool {
  996      // If the surface is in read-only mode, always require confirmation
  997      if (self.readonly) return true;
  998  
  999      // If the child has exited, then our process is certainly not alive.
 1000      // We check this first to avoid the locking overhead below.
 1001      if (self.child_exited) return false;
 1002  
 1003      // Check the configuration for confirming close behavior.
 1004      return switch (self.config.confirm_close_surface) {
 1005          .always => true,
 1006          .false => false,
 1007          .true => true: {
 1008              self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
 1009              defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
 1010              break :true !self.io.terminal.cursorIsAtPrompt();
 1011          },
 1012      };
 1013  }
n42 src/terminal/Terminal.zig:2941-2954
 2941  pub fn cursorIsAtPrompt(self: *Terminal) bool {
 2942      // If we're on the secondary screen, we're never at a prompt.
 2943      if (self.screens.active_key == .alternate) return false;
 2944  
 2945      // If our page row is a prompt then we're always at a prompt
 2946      const cursor: *const Screen.Cursor = &self.screens.active.cursor;
 2947      if (cursor.page_row.semantic_prompt != .none) return true;
 2948  
 2949      // Otherwise, determine our cursor state
 2950      return switch (cursor.semantic_content) {
 2951          .input, .prompt => true,
 2952          .output => false,
 2953      };
 2954  }

n42 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:786-820
  786      fn surfaceCloseRequest(
  787          surface: *Surface,
  788          self: *Self,
  789      ) callconv(.c) void {
  790          const core = surface.core() orelse return;
  791  
  792          // Reset our pending close state
  793          const priv = self.private();
  794          priv.pending_close = null;
  795  
  796          // Find the surface in the tree to verify this is valid and
  797          // set our pending close handle.
  798          const tree = self.getTree() orelse return;
  799          priv.pending_close = tree.locate(surface) orelse return;

  803              closeConfirmationClose(
  804                  null,
  805                  self,
  806              );
  807              return;
  808          }
  809  
  810          // Show a confirmation dialog
  811          const dialog: *CloseConfirmationDialog = .new(.surface);
  812          _ = CloseConfirmationDialog.signals.@"close-request".connect(
  813              dialog,
  814              *Self,
  815              closeConfirmationClose,
  816              self,
  817              .{},
  818          );
  819          dialog.present(self.as(gtk.Widget));
  820      }

n43 src/Surface.zig:829-879
  829  pub fn deinit(self: *Surface) void {
  830      // Stop search thread
  831      if (self.search) |*s| s.deinit();
  832  
  833      // Stop rendering thread
  834      {
  835          self.renderer_thread.stop.notify() catch |err|
  836              log.err("error notifying renderer thread to stop, may stall err={}", .{err});
  837          self.renderer_thr.join();
  838      }
  839  
  840      // Stop our IO thread
  841      {
  842          self.io_thread.stop.notify() catch |err|
  843              log.err("error notifying io thread to stop, may stall err={}", .{err});
  844          self.io_thr.join();
  845      }
  846  
  847      // We need to deinit AFTER everything is stopped, since there are
  848      // shared values between the two threads.
  849      //
  850      // The renderer must be deinitialized before the renderer thread:
  851      // on macOS the renderer's display link callback points at the
  852      // thread's `draw_now` handle, and the renderer's deinit is what
  853      // stops the display link for good.
  854      self.renderer.deinit();
  855      self.renderer_thread.deinit();
  856      self.io_thread.deinit();
  857      self.mouse.selection_gesture.deinit(&self.io.terminal);
  858      self.io.deinit();
  859  
  860      if (self.inspector) |v| {
  861          v.deinit(self.alloc);
  862          self.alloc.destroy(v);
  863      }
  864  
  865      // Clean up our keyboard state
  866      for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |req| req.deinit();
  867      self.keyboard.sequence_queued.deinit(self.alloc);
  868      self.keyboard.table_stack.deinit(self.alloc);
  869  
  870      // Clean up our font grid
  871      self.app.font_grid_set.deref(self.font_grid_key);
  872  
  873      // Clean up our render state
  874      if (self.renderer_state.preedit) |p| self.alloc.free(p.codepoints);
  875      self.alloc.destroy(self.renderer_state.mutex);
  876      self.config.deinit();
  877  
  878      log.info("surface closed id={x}", .{self.id});
  879  }

n43 src/App.zig:222-252
  222  pub fn deleteSurface(self: *App, rt_surface: *apprt.Surface) void {
  223      // If this surface is the focused surface then we need to clear it.
  224      // There was a bug where we relied on hasSurface to return false and
  225      // just let focused surface be but the allocator was reusing addresses
  226      // after free and giving false positives, so we must clear it.
  227      if (self.focused_surface) |focused| {
  228          if (focused == rt_surface.core()) {
  229              self.focused_surface = null;
  230          }
  231      }
  232  
  233      var i: usize = 0;
  234      while (i < self.surfaces.items.len) {
  235          if (self.surfaces.items[i] == rt_surface) {
  236              _ = self.surfaces.swapRemove(i);
  237              continue;
  238          }
  239  
  240          i += 1;
  241      }
  242  
  243      // If we have no surfaces, we can start the quit timer. It is up to the
  244      // apprt to determine if this is necessary.
  245      if (self.surfaces.items.len == 0) _ = rt_surface.rtApp().performAction(
  246          .app,
  247          .quit_timer,
  248          .start,
  249      ) catch |err| {
  250          log.warn("error starting quit timer err={}", .{err});
  251      };
  252  }

n43 src/apprt/gtk/class/surface.zig:1963-1981
 1963      fn finalize(self: *Self) callconv(.c) void {
 1964          const alloc = Application.default().allocator();
 1965          const priv = self.private();
 1966          if (priv.core_surface) |v| {
 1967              // Remove ourselves from the list of known surfaces in the app.
 1968              // We do this before deinit in case a callback triggers
 1969              // searching for this surface.
 1970              Application.default().core().deleteSurface(self.rt());
 1971  
 1972              // NOTE: We must deinit the surface in the finalize call and NOT
 1973              // the dispose call because the inspector widget relies on this
 1974              // behavior with a weakRef to properly deactivate.
 1975  
 1976              // Deinit the surface
 1977              v.deinit();
 1978              alloc.destroy(v);
 1979  
 1980              priv.core_surface = null;
 1981          }

  498          const tree = self.getTree() orelse return;
  499          var it = tree.iterator();
  500          while (it.next()) |entry| {
  501              const surface = entry.view;
  502              _ = Surface.signals.@"close-request".connect(
  503                  surface,
  504                  *Self,
  505                  surfaceCloseRequest,
  506                  self,
  507                  .{},
  508              );
n43 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:604-639
  604      pub fn setTree(self: *Self, tree_: ?*const Surface.Tree) void {
  605          const priv = self.private();
  606  
  607          // We always normalize our tree parameter so that empty trees
  608          // become null so that we don't have to deal with callers being
  609          // confused about that.
  610          const tree: ?*const Surface.Tree = tree: {
  611              const tree = tree_ orelse break :tree null;
  612              if (tree.isEmpty()) break :tree null;
  613              break :tree tree;
  614          };
  615  
  616          // Emit the signal so that handlers can witness both the before and
  617          // after values of the tree.
  618          signals.changed.impl.emit(
  619              self,
  620              null,
  621              .{ priv.tree, tree },
  622              null,
  623          );
  624  
  625          if (priv.tree) |old_tree| {
  626              self.disconnectSurfaceHandlers();
  627              ext.boxedFree(Surface.Tree, old_tree);
  628              priv.tree = null;
  629          }
  630  
  631          if (tree) |new_tree| {
  632              assert(priv.tree == null);
  633              assert(!new_tree.isEmpty());
  634              priv.tree = ext.boxedCopy(Surface.Tree, new_tree);
  635              self.connectSurfaceHandlers();
  636          }
  637  
  638          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.tree.impl.param_spec);
  639      }
n43 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:786-871
  786      fn surfaceCloseRequest(
  787          surface: *Surface,
  788          self: *Self,
  789      ) callconv(.c) void {
  790          const core = surface.core() orelse return;
  791  
  792          // Reset our pending close state
  793          const priv = self.private();
  794          priv.pending_close = null;
  795  
  796          // Find the surface in the tree to verify this is valid and
  797          // set our pending close handle.
  798          const tree = self.getTree() orelse return;
  799          priv.pending_close = tree.locate(surface) orelse return;
  800  
  801          // If we don't need to confirm then just close immediately.
  802          if (!core.needsConfirmQuit()) {
  803              closeConfirmationClose(
  804                  null,
  805                  self,
  806              );
  807              return;
  808          }
  809  
  810          // Show a confirmation dialog
  811          const dialog: *CloseConfirmationDialog = .new(.surface);
  812          _ = CloseConfirmationDialog.signals.@"close-request".connect(
  813              dialog,
  814              *Self,
  815              closeConfirmationClose,
  816              self,
  817              .{},
  818          );
  819          dialog.present(self.as(gtk.Widget));
  820      }
  821  
  822      fn closeConfirmationClose(
  823          _: ?*CloseConfirmationDialog,
  824          self: *Self,
  825      ) callconv(.c) void {
  826          // Get the handle we're closing
  827          const priv = self.private();
  828          const handle = priv.pending_close orelse return;
  829          priv.pending_close = null;
  830  
  831          self.removeHandle(handle) catch |err| {
  832              log.warn("unable to remove surface from tree: {}", .{err});
  833          };
  834      }
  835  
  836      /// Remove the node with the given handle from the tree, making the
  837      /// surface next to it the last focused surface of the tree.
  838      fn removeHandle(
  839          self: *Self,
  840          handle: Surface.Tree.Node.Handle,
  841      ) Allocator.Error!void {
  842          // Figure out our next focus target. The next focus target is
  843          // always the "previous" surface unless we're the leftmost then
  844          // its the next.
  845          const old_tree = self.getTree() orelse return;
  846          const next_focus: ?*Surface = next_focus: {
  847              const alloc = Application.default().allocator();
  848              const next_handle: Surface.Tree.Node.Handle =
  849                  (old_tree.goto(alloc, handle, .previous) catch null) orelse
  850                  (old_tree.goto(alloc, handle, .next) catch null) orelse
  851                  break :next_focus null;
  852              if (next_handle == handle) break :next_focus null;
  853  
  854              // Note: we don't need to ref this or anything because its
  855              // guaranteed to remain in the new tree since its not part
  856              // of the handle we're removing.
  857              break :next_focus old_tree.nodes[next_handle.idx()].leaf;
  858          };
  859  
  860          // Remove it from the tree.
  861          var new_tree = try old_tree.remove(
  862              Application.default().allocator(),
  863              handle,
  864          );
  865          defer new_tree.deinit();
  866          self.setTree(&new_tree);
  867  
  868          // Grab focus. We have to set this on the "last focused" because our
  869          // focus will be set when the tree is redrawn.
  870          if (next_focus) |v| self.private().last_focused.set(v);
  871      }
n43 src/apprt/gtk/class/split_tree.zig:906-986
  906      fn propTree(
  907          self: *Self,
  908          _: *gobject.ParamSpec,
  909          _: ?*anyopaque,
  910      ) callconv(.c) void {
  911          const priv = self.private();
  912  
  913          // No matter what we notify
  914          self.as(gobject.Object).freezeNotify();
  915          defer self.as(gobject.Object).thawNotify();
  916          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.@"has-surfaces".impl.param_spec);
  917          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.@"is-zoomed".impl.param_spec);
  918  
  919          // If we were planning a rebuild or focus restore, always remove
  920          // that so we can start from a clean slate.
  921          if (priv.rebuild_source) |v| {
  922              if (glib.Source.remove(v) == 0) {
  923                  log.warn("unable to remove rebuild source", .{});
  924              }
  925              priv.rebuild_source = null;
  926          }
  927          if (priv.restore_focus_source) |v| {
  928              if (glib.Source.remove(v) == 0) {
  929                  log.warn("unable to remove restore_focus source", .{});
  930              }
  931              priv.restore_focus_source = null;
  932          }
  933  
  934          // If we transitioned to an empty tree, clear immediately instead of
  935          // waiting for an idle callback. Delaying teardown can keep the last
  936          // surface alive during shutdown if the main loop exits first.
  937          if (priv.tree == null) {
  938              priv.tree_bin.setChild(null);
  939              return;
  940          }
  941  
  942          // Build on an idle callback so rapid tree changes are debounced.
  943          // We keep the existing tree attached until the rebuild runs,
  944          // which avoids transient empty frames.
  945          assert(priv.rebuild_source == null);
  946          priv.rebuild_source = glib.idleAdd(
  947              onRebuild,
  948              self,
  949          );
  950      }
  951  
  952      fn onRebuild(ud: ?*anyopaque) callconv(.c) c_int {
  953          const self: *Self = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ud orelse return 0));
  954  
  955          // Always mark our rebuild source as null since we're done.
  956          const priv = self.private();
  957          priv.rebuild_source = null;
  958  
  959          // Rebuild our tree
  960          const tree: *const Surface.Tree = self.private().tree orelse &.empty;
  961          if (tree.isEmpty()) {
  962              priv.tree_bin.setChild(null);
  963          } else {
  964              const built = self.buildTree(
  965                  tree,
  966                  tree.zoomed orelse .root,
  967              );
  968              defer built.deinit();
  969              priv.tree_bin.setChild(built.widget);
  970          }
  971  
  972          // Replacing our tree widget hierarchy can reset focus state.
  973          // If we have a last-focused surface, restore focus to it.
  974          if (priv.last_focused.get()) |v| {
  975              defer v.unref();
  976              v.grabFocus();
  977          }
  978  
  979          // Our split status may have changed
  980          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.@"is-split".impl.param_spec);
  981  
  982          // Our active surface may have changed
  983          self.as(gobject.Object).notifyByPspec(properties.@"active-surface".impl.param_spec);
  984  
  985          return 0;
  986      }

n43 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:469-495
  469      func closeSurface(
  470          _ node: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.Node,
  471          withConfirmation: Bool = true
  472      ) {
  473          // This node must be part of our tree
  474          guard surfaceTree.contains(node) else { return }
  475  
  476          // If the child process is not alive, then we exit immediately
  477          guard withConfirmation else {
  478              removeSurfaceNode(node)
  479              return
  480          }
  481  
  482          // Confirm close. We use an NSAlert instead of a SwiftUI confirmationDialog
  483          // due to SwiftUI bugs (see Ghostty #560). To repeat from #560, the bug is that
  484          // confirmationDialog allows the user to Cmd-W close the alert, but when doing
  485          // so SwiftUI does not update any of the bindings to note that window is no longer
  486          // being shown, and provides no callback to detect this.
  487          confirmClose(
  488              messageText: "Close Terminal?",
  489              informativeText: "The terminal still has a running process. If you close the terminal the process will be killed."
  490          ) { [weak self] in
  491              if let self {
  492                  self.removeSurfaceNode(node)
  493              }
  494          }
  495      }
n43 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:518-583
  518      private func removeSurfaceNode(_ node: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.Node) {
  519          // Move focus if the closed surface was focused and we have a next target
  520          let nextFocus: Ghostty.SurfaceView? = if node.contains(
  521              where: { $0 == focusedSurface }
  522          ) {
  523              findNextFocusTargetAfterClosing(node: node)
  524          } else {
  525              nil
  526          }
  527  
  528          replaceSurfaceTree(
  529              surfaceTree.removing(node),
  530              // When a non-focused surface is removed and this window stays as the key window,
  531              // we should refocus the `focusedSurface` to make sure the window's firstResponder remains as it is.
  532              //
  533              // This is a weird workaround, since `resignFirstResponder` wasn't called on `focusedSurface` after drag,
  534              // but the first responder became the window itself.
  535              moveFocusTo: nextFocus ?? focusedSurface,
  536              undoAction: "Close Terminal"
  537          )
  538      }
  539  
  540      func replaceSurfaceTree(
  541          _ newTree: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>,
  542          moveFocusTo newView: Ghostty.SurfaceView? = nil,
  543          moveFocusFrom oldView: Ghostty.SurfaceView? = nil,
  544          undoAction: String? = nil
  545      ) {
  546          // Setup our new split tree
  547          let oldTree = surfaceTree
  548          surfaceTree = newTree
  549          if let newView {
  550              DispatchQueue.main.async {
  551                  Ghostty.moveFocus(to: newView, from: oldView)
  552              }
  553          }
  554  
  555          // Setup our undo
  556          guard let undoManager else { return }
  557          if let undoAction {
  558              undoManager.setActionName(undoAction)
  559          }
  560  
  561          undoManager.registerUndo(
  562              withTarget: self,
  563              expiresAfter: undoExpiration
  564          ) { target in
  565              target.surfaceTree = oldTree
  566              if let oldView {
  567                  DispatchQueue.main.async {
  568                      Ghostty.moveFocus(to: oldView, from: target.focusedSurface)
  569                  }
  570              }
  571  
  572              undoManager.registerUndo(
  573                  withTarget: target,
  574                  expiresAfter: target.undoExpiration
  575              ) { target in
  576                  target.replaceSurfaceTree(
  577                      newTree,
  578                      moveFocusTo: newView,
  579                      moveFocusFrom: target.focusedSurface,
  580                      undoAction: undoAction)
  581              }
  582          }
  583      }
n43 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/BaseTerminalController.swift:658-664
  658      @objc private func ghosttyDidCloseSurface(_ notification: Notification) {
  659          guard let target = notification.object as? Ghostty.SurfaceView else { return }
  660          guard let node = surfaceTree.root?.node(view: target) else { return }
  661          closeSurface(
  662              node,
  663              withConfirmation: (notification.userInfo?["process_alive"] as? Bool) ?? false)
  664      }
n43 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/TerminalController.swift:695-755
  695      override func closeSurface(
  696          _ node: SplitTree<Ghostty.SurfaceView>.Node,
  697          withConfirmation: Bool = true
  698      ) {
  699          // If this isn't the root then we're dealing with a split closure.
  700          if surfaceTree.root != node {
  701              super.closeSurface(node, withConfirmation: withConfirmation)
  702              return
  703          }
  704  
  705          // More than 1 window means we have tabs and we're closing a tab
  706          if window?.tabGroup?.windows.count ?? 0 > 1 {
  707              if withConfirmation {
  708                  closeTab(nil)
  709              } else {
  710                  closeTabImmediately()
  711              }
  712              return
  713          }
  714  
  715          // 1 window, closing the window
  716          if withConfirmation {
  717              closeWindow(nil)
  718          } else {
  719              closeWindowImmediately()
  720          }
  721      }
  722  
  723      func closeTabImmediately(registerRedo: Bool = true) {
  724          guard let window = window else { return }
  725          guard let tabGroup = window.tabGroup,
  726                  tabGroup.windows.count > 1 else {
  727              closeWindowImmediately()
  728              return
  729          }
  730  
  731          cancelPendingInitialPresentation()
  732  
  733          // Undo
  734          if let undoManager, let undoState {
  735              // Register undo action to restore the tab
  736              undoManager.setActionName("Close Tab")
  737              undoManager.registerUndo(
  738                  withTarget: ghostty,
  739                  expiresAfter: undoExpiration
  740              ) { ghostty in
  741                  let newController = TerminalController(ghostty, with: undoState)
  742  
  743                  if registerRedo {
  744                      undoManager.registerUndo(
  745                          withTarget: newController,
  746                          expiresAfter: newController.undoExpiration
  747                      ) { target in
  748                          target.closeTabImmediately()
  749                      }
  750                  }
  751              }
  752          }
  753  
  754          window.close()
  755      }
n43 macos/Sources/Features/Terminal/TerminalController.swift:848-870
  848      func closeWindowImmediately() {
  849          guard let window = window else { return }
  850  
  851          cancelPendingInitialPresentation()
  852  
  853          registerUndoForCloseWindow()
  854  
  855          if let tabGroup = window.tabGroup, tabGroup.windows.count > 1 {
  856              tabGroup.windows.forEach { window in
  857                  // Clear out the surfacetree to ensure there is no undo state.
  858                  // This prevents unnecessary undos registered since AppKit may
  859                  // process them on later ticks so we can't just disable undo registration.
  860                  if let controller = window.windowController as? TerminalController {
  861                      controller.cancelPendingInitialPresentation()
  862                      controller.surfaceTree = .init()
  863                  }
  864  
  865                  window.close()
  866              }
  867          } else {
  868              window.close()
  869          }
  870      }
